## 603. NATIONAL CENTRE FOR THE CONTROL OF SMALL ARMS AND LIGHT WEAPONS (NCCSALW)

**MDA Code:** 157005001

National Centre for the Control of Small Arms and Light Weapons is federal agency coordinating control and regulation of illicit proliferation of small arms and light weapons. Operating under Office of National Security Adviser. Established to address proliferation of illegal firearms threatening national security. Coordinates national action on small arms control implementing ECOWAS Convention. Conducts weapons collection and destruction programs. Monitors cross-border weapons trafficking. Collaborates with security agencies on arms control enforcement. Conducts awareness campaigns on dangers of illicit weapons. Supports disarmament programs in conflict-affected areas. Coordinates stockpile management preventing leakage from government arsenals. Addresses armed banditry, kidnapping and insurgency fueled by weapons proliferation. Collaborates with neighboring countries on transborder arms control. Engages civil society on peacebuilding and weapons reduction. Addresses challenges from porous borders, armed groups and limited enforcement capacity. Proliferation of AK-47s and other weapons enables banditry devastating northwestern Nigeria, farmer-herder conflicts and insurgency. Critical for security, conflict prevention and controlling weapons fueling violence.

**Core Mandate:** To coordinate control of small arms and light weapons, prevent illicit proliferation, support disarmament programs and reduce weapons fueling insecurity, banditry and conflicts.

**Statutory Functions:**
(a) Coordinate national policy on small arms and light weapons control
(b) Implement ECOWAS Convention on Small Arms and Light Weapons
(c) Conduct weapons collection programs in communities
(d) Organize destruction of seized and surrendered weapons
(e) Monitor cross-border trafficking of illegal firearms
(f) Collaborate with Customs on border arms control
(g) Coordinate stockpile management preventing arsenal leakage
(h) Address proliferation fueling armed banditry
(i) Combat weapons enabling kidnapping gangs
(j) Support disarmament in conflict-affected areas
(k) Conduct public awareness on dangers of illicit weapons
(l) Engage communities on voluntary weapons surrender
(m) Coordinate security agencies' arms control efforts
(n) Collaborate with neighboring countries on transborder control
(o) Address farmer-herder conflicts involving weapons
(p) Support DDR (Disarmament, Demobilization, Reintegration) programs
(q) Monitor arms flows from Libya and Sahel region
(r) Engage civil society on peacebuilding initiatives
(s) Conduct research on weapons proliferation patterns
(t) Advance security and conflict prevention through small arms control addressing proliferation of AK-47s and weapons fueling northwestern banditry, kidnapping and insurgency

## 615. NATIONAL COUNTER TERRORISM CENTRE (NCTC)

**MDA Code:** 157006001

National Counter Terrorism Centre is federal agency coordinating counter-terrorism strategy and operations. Operating under Office of National Security Adviser. Established to coordinate multi-agency counter-terrorism efforts against Boko Haram, ISWAP and other terrorist groups. Develops national counter-terrorism strategy and policies. Coordinates military, police, DSS and security agencies' counter-terrorism operations. Conducts terrorism threat analysis and intelligence assessments. Monitors terrorist activities and radicalization. Coordinates de-radicalization and counter-violent extremism programs. Addresses terrorism financing and fundraising. Promotes community engagement in counter-terrorism. Supports victims of terrorism. Coordinates regional counter-terrorism cooperation with Lake Chad Basin countries. Collaborates with international counter-terrorism partners. Addresses Boko Haram insurgency that killed 35,000+ and displaced millions since 2009. Counters ISWAP (Islamic State West Africa Province) operating in northeastern Nigeria. Addresses emerging terrorist threats and kidnapping-for-ransom groups. Critical for national security, coordinating fight against terrorism and protecting Nigerians from terrorist violence.

**Core Mandate:** To coordinate counter-terrorism strategy, integrate security agencies' operations, counter violent extremism, address terrorism financing and lead Nigeria's fight against Boko Haram and terrorist groups.

**Statutory Functions:**
(a) Coordinate national counter-terrorism strategy and operations
(b) Develop counter-terrorism policies and frameworks
(c) Integrate military, police and intelligence agencies' counter-terrorism efforts
(d) Coordinate operations against Boko Haram insurgency
(e) Counter ISWAP (Islamic State West Africa Province) terrorism
(f) Conduct terrorism threat analysis and risk assessments
(g) Monitor terrorist activities, recruitment and radicalization
(h) Coordinate intelligence sharing among security agencies
(i) Address terrorism financing and fundraising networks
(j) Disrupt terrorist supply chains and logistics
(k) Develop de-radicalization programs for former combatants
(l) Implement counter-violent extremism (CVE) programs
(m) Promote community resilience against radicalization
(n) Support victims of terrorism and terrorist attacks
(o) Coordinate regional counter-terrorism with Chad, Niger, Cameroon
(p) Participate in Multinational Joint Task Force (MNJTF)
(q) Collaborate with international counter-terrorism partners
(r) Address kidnapping-for-ransom terrorist groups
(s) Counter terrorism propaganda and recruitment
(t) Advance national security coordinating fight against Boko Haram, ISWAP and terrorism protecting Nigerians from terrorist violence that killed thousands and displaced millions

## 616. NATIONAL CYBERCRIME COORDINATION CENTRE (NCCC)

**MDA Code:** 157007001

National Cybercrime Coordination Centre is federal agency coordinating cybersecurity and combating cybercrime. Operating under Office of National Security Adviser. Established to coordinate national response to cyber threats, cybercrime and cyber attacks. Coordinates cybersecurity across critical infrastructure sectors. Monitors cyber threats and cyber attacks. Investigates cybercrimes including online fraud, hacking and identity theft. Addresses "Yahoo Yahoo" (internet fraud/419 scams) damaging Nigeria's reputation. Combats Business Email Compromise scams targeting businesses globally. Coordinates cybersecurity incident response. Promotes cybersecurity awareness and safe internet practices. Collaborates with EFCC on internet fraud investigations. Coordinates with international cybercrime agencies (FBI, Interpol). Monitors dark web activities and cyber criminal networks. Addresses challenges from sophisticated cyber criminals, limited cyber forensics capacity and international jurisdictional issues. Nigeria notorious for internet fraud schemes with fraudsters (Yahoo Boys) stealing billions globally. Critical for cybersecurity, protecting critical infrastructure, combating cybercrime and improving Nigeria's international reputation.

**Core Mandate:** To coordinate cybersecurity, combat cybercrime including internet fraud, protect critical infrastructure, coordinate cyber incident response and improve Nigeria's cyber defense capabilities.

**Statutory Functions:**
(a) Coordinate national cybersecurity strategy and cybercrime response
(b) Monitor cyber threats and cyber attacks on Nigerian systems
(c) Coordinate cybersecurity for critical infrastructure sectors
(d) Investigate cybercrimes including hacking and data breaches
(e) Combat internet fraud and "Yahoo Yahoo" scams
(f) Address Business Email Compromise (BEC) scams
(g) Investigate identity theft and online financial fraud
(h) Coordinate cybersecurity incident response

(k) Promote cybersecurity awareness and education

(m) Develop cyber forensics capabilities
(n) Collaborate with EFCC on internet fraud cases
(o) Coordinate with FBI, Interpol on international cybercrime
(p) Address ransomware attacks on organizations
(q) Protect government networks from cyber espionage
(r) Regulate critical infrastructure cybersecurity
(s) Support private sector cybersecurity resilience
(t) Advance cybersecurity and combat cybercrime protecting critical infrastructure and improving Nigeria's reputation damaged by internet fraud through coordinated response to cyber threats

Police Service Commission is independent constitutional federal body responsible for Nigeria Police Force personnel management. Operates under 1999 Constitution Third Schedule Part I. Recruits, appoints, promotes and disciplines police officers from Constable to Inspector-General rank. Ensures merit-based professional police force independent of political interference.

Coordinates federal government business and operations. Supervises federal ministries, departments and agencies. Manages Federal Executive Council secretariat.

## 634. NATIONAL IDENTITY MANAGEMENT COMMISSION (NIMC)

**MDA Code:** 161005001

National Identity Management Commission is federal agency managing national identity database and identity cards. Headquartered in Abuja with enrollment centers nationwide, operating under Federal Ministry of Communications and Digital Economy. Established to create unified national identity database and issue National Identification Numbers (NIN). Issues National Identification Number (NIN) - unique lifetime identifier. Conducts biometric enrollment capturing fingerprints, photograph and iris. Issues national identity cards to Nigerians. Maintains National Identity Database storing citizens' biodata. Supports SIM-NIN linkage policy requiring phone number registration with NIN. Promotes digital identity and e-governance. Provides identity verification services to government and private sector. Supports financial inclusion through identity provision. Addresses identity fraud and impersonation. Integrates with BVN (Bank Verification Number) system. Faces massive challenges - enrollment backlogs, limited enrollment centers, card production delays (enrolled but awaiting cards), server downtime and public frustration. 2022 SIM-NIN linkage deadline caused chaos with millions rushing for NIN. Over 90 million Nigerians enrolled but many awaiting cards. Critical for identity management, security, financial inclusion and e-governance despite operational challenges.

**Core Mandate:** To create national identity database, issue National Identification Numbers, enroll Nigerians biometrically, produce identity cards and provide identity verification services.

**Statutory Functions:**
(a) Create and maintain National Identity Database for all Nigerians
(b) Issue National Identification Number (NIN) - unique lifetime identifier
(c) Conduct biometric enrollment capturing fingerprints and photograph
(d) Capture iris biometrics for identity verification
(e) Issue national identity cards to enrolled Nigerians
(f) Produce and distribute identity cards nationwide
(g) Maintain centralized identity database
(h) Support SIM-NIN linkage policy for phone registration
(i) Provide identity verification services to government agencies
(j) Support financial inclusion through identity provision
(k) Integrate with Bank Verification Number (BVN) system
(l) Address identity fraud and impersonation
(m) Promote digital identity and e-governance
(n) Operate enrollment centers nationwide
(o) Conduct mass enrollment campaigns
(p) Provide identity verification API for private sector
(q) Support voter registration through identity verification
(r) Address enrollment backlogs (90M+ enrolled, many awaiting cards)
(s) Improve server capacity and enrollment infrastructure
(t) Advance digital identity and inclusion managing national database despite challenges of enrollment backlogs, card production delays and infrastructure constraints serving 200M+ population

# NIGERIAN FEDERAL AGENCY MANDATES: AGENCIES 701-720

## 701. NEW PARTNERSHIP FOR AFRICA'S DEVELOPMENT (NEPAD) - NIGERIA

**MDA Code:** 161009001

NEPAD Nigeria Office is national coordinating body for New Partnership for Africa's Development continental initiative. Operating under Office of President. Established to coordinate Nigeria's participation in NEPAD - African Union's development framework. Coordinates NEPAD programs and projects in Nigeria. Promotes regional integration and African development. Implements NEPAD's priority areas - agriculture, infrastructure, governance, human development. Coordinates infrastructure development aligned with NEPAD objectives. Promotes intra-African trade and economic cooperation. Supports agriculture and food security initiatives. Collaborates with African Union and regional bodies. Promotes peer review mechanism for governance standards. Coordinates with ECOWAS on regional integration. Addresses challenges from limited funding, coordination gaps with MDAs and African development priorities competing with national focus. NEPAD represents Africa's self-driven development agenda. Nigeria's leadership role in African affairs. Critical for continental development participation, regional cooperation and advancing Africa's development through Nigerian engagement in NEPAD programs.

**Core Mandate:** To coordinate Nigeria's NEPAD participation, promote African development initiatives, support regional integration and implement NEPAD priority programs.

**Statutory Functions:**
(a) Coordinate Nigeria's participation in New Partnership for Africa's Development
(b) Implement NEPAD programs and projects in Nigeria
(c) Promote regional integration and African economic development
(d) Coordinate agriculture and food security initiatives
(e) Support infrastructure development aligned with NEPAD priorities
(f) Promote intra-African trade and economic cooperation
(g) Coordinate human development and capacity building programs
(h) Implement governance and democracy initiatives
(i) Promote African Peer Review Mechanism (APRM) for governance
(j) Collaborate with African Union on continental programs
(k) Coordinate with ECOWAS on West African regional integration
(l) Support science, technology and innovation development
(m) Promote climate change adaptation and environmental sustainability
(n) Coordinate with federal MDAs on NEPAD alignment
(o) Mobilize resources for NEPAD projects
(p) Monitor Nigeria's progress on NEPAD targets
(q) Promote South-South cooperation within Africa
(r) Engage development partners supporting African development
(s) Advance Nigeria's leadership role in African affairs
(t) Promote continental development through NEPAD coordination supporting regional integration, infrastructure and agriculture advancing Africa's self-driven development agenda

## 584. NATIONAL AGENCY FOR THE CONTROL OF AIDS (NACA)

**MDA Code:** 161012001

National Agency for the Control of AIDS (NACA) is federal coordinating agency managing Nigeria's HIV/AIDS response. Headquartered in Abuja with state branches, operating under Office of President (previously under Federal Ministry of Health). Established to coordinate multi-sectoral HIV/AIDS prevention, treatment and care programs. Develops national HIV/AIDS policy and strategic frameworks. Coordinates HIV prevention programs including behavior change campaigns. Manages antiretroviral therapy (ART) program providing free HIV treatment. Supports HIV testing and counseling services. Prevents mother-to-child HIV transmission through PMTCT programs. Coordinates AIDS impact mitigation programs supporting orphans and vulnerable children. Manages Global Fund and PEPFAR HIV resources. Conducts HIV surveillance and epidemiological monitoring. Promotes stigma reduction and human rights for people living with HIV. Coordinates state AIDS control agencies (SACAs). Collaborates with WHO, UNAIDS, development partners and civil society. Nigeria has significant HIV burden (~1.9 million people living with HIV, 3rd globally). Addresses challenges from funding gaps, stigma and achieving UNAIDS 95-95-95 targets. Critical for HIV epidemic control and public health.

**Core Mandate:** To coordinate national HIV/AIDS response, manage prevention and treatment programs, reduce new infections, ensure treatment access and control AIDS epidemic in Nigeria.

**Statutory Functions:**
(a) Coordinate multi-sectoral national HIV/AIDS response programs
(b) Develop national HIV/AIDS policy and strategic frameworks
(c) Manage antiretroviral therapy (ART) program providing free treatment
(d) Coordinate HIV prevention programs and behavior change campaigns
(e) Promote HIV testing and counseling services nationwide
(f) Prevent mother-to-child HIV transmission through PMTCT programs
(g) Support orphans and vulnerable children affected by AIDS
(h) Manage Global Fund to Fight AIDS, TB and Malaria resources
(i) Coordinate PEPFAR (US President's Emergency Plan) HIV funding
(j) Conduct HIV surveillance and epidemiological monitoring
(k) Track progress toward UNAIDS 95-95-95 targets
(l) Promote stigma reduction and rights-based approaches
(m) Coordinate State AIDS Control Agencies (SACAs) in 36 states
(n) Support civil society and community-based AIDS organizations
(o) Conduct HIV awareness and prevention education campaigns
(p) Promote condom distribution and harm reduction
(q) Address key populations (sex workers, MSM, PWID) vulnerabilities
(r) Collaborate with WHO, UNAIDS and development partners
(s) Monitor HIV drug resistance and treatment outcomes
(t) Advance HIV epidemic control managing response for 1.9M people living with HIV achieving viral suppression, preventing new infections and supporting affected communities

## 671. NATIONAL PENSION COMMISSION (PenCom)

**MDA Code:** 161013001

National Pension Commission is federal regulatory agency overseeing Nigeria's Contributory Pension Scheme. Headquartered in Abuja, operating under Pension Reform Act 2014. Established to regulate pension industry, protect pension fund assets and ensure retirement income security. Regulates Contributory Pension Scheme for public and private sector workers. Licenses and supervises Pension Fund Administrators (PFAs) managing pension contributions. Regulates Pension Fund Custodians (PFCs) safeguarding pension assets. Enforces mandatory pension contributions (18% - 10% employer, 8% employee). Maintains database of pension contributors and Retirement Savings Accounts (RSAs). Approves pension fund investments ensuring safety and returns. Addresses employer non-remittance of pension deductions. Promotes pension coverage expansion. Regulates Micro Pension Plan for informal sector workers. Collaborates with NSITF on workplace pensions. Pension assets exceed N15 trillion managed by PFAs. Faces challenges from employer non-compliance (many employers deduct but don't remit), limited informal sector coverage and investment returns concerns. Pension reform transformed Nigeria from pay-as-you-go to funded system. Critical for retirement security, pension assets protection and ensuring workers have income in old age.

**Core Mandate:** To regulate Contributory Pension Scheme, supervise pension operators, protect pension assets, ensure compliance, expand coverage and secure workers' retirement income.

**Statutory Functions:**
(a) Regulate Contributory Pension Scheme for public and private sector
(b) License and supervise Pension Fund Administrators (PFAs)
(c) Regulate Pension Fund Custodians (PFCs) safeguarding assets
(d) Enforce mandatory pension contributions (18% of salary)
(e) Maintain database of pension contributors nationwide
(f) Register Retirement Savings Accounts (RSAs) for workers
(g) Approve pension fund investment guidelines
(h) Monitor pension fund investments ensuring safety and returns
(i) Address employer non-remittance of pension deductions
(j) Sanction employers failing to remit pension contributions
(k) Promote pension coverage expansion
(l) Regulate Micro Pension Plan for informal sector workers
(m) Protect pension fund assets (N15+ trillion managed)
(n) Ensure pension payments to retirees
(o) Monitor PFA compliance with regulations
(p) Address contributor complaints and disputes
(q) Promote pension awareness and education
(r) Collaborate with NSITF on workplace pensions
(s) Monitor demographic trends affecting pension sustainability
(t) Advance retirement security regulating Contributory Pension Scheme protecting N15+ trillion pension assets ensuring workers' retirement income despite employer non-compliance challenges

## 628. NATIONAL HAJJ COMMISSION OF NIGERIA (NAHCON)

**MDA Code:** 161014001

National Hajj Commission of Nigeria is federal agency regulating and coordinating Hajj pilgrimage to Saudi Arabia. Headquartered in Abuja, operating under Office of President. Established to organize, regulate and supervise Nigerian Muslims' annual Hajj pilgrimage to Mecca. Coordinates with Saudi Arabian authorities on Hajj arrangements. Registers Nigerian pilgrims and processes Hajj documentation. Negotiates Hajj quotas for Nigeria with Saudi Arabia. Provides Hajj orientation and education for pilgrims. Regulates Hajj tour operators and travel agents. Airlifts pilgrims to Saudi Arabia and back. Provides accommodation and welfare services in Mecca and Medina. Manages medical services for pilgrims. Ensures pilgrims' safety and security. Regulates Hajj costs preventing exploitation. Addresses challenges from high Hajj costs, limited quotas, logistics complexity and pilgrim welfare concerns. Nigeria sends 95,000+ pilgrims annually (within Saudi quota). Hajj is fifth pillar of Islam obligatory for able Muslims. Hajj operations involve billions of Naira. Critical for religious fulfillment, pilgrim protection and managing mass pilgrimage logistics.

**Core Mandate:** To organize and regulate Hajj pilgrimage, coordinate with Saudi authorities, register pilgrims, ensure pilgrim welfare and manage Nigerian Muslims' annual pilgrimage to Mecca.

**Statutory Functions:**
(a) Organize and coordinate Nigerian Muslims' Hajj pilgrimage to Mecca
(b) Register Nigerian pilgrims for annual Hajj
(c) Process Hajj documentation and Saudi visas
(d) Coordinate with Saudi Arabian Hajj authorities
(e) Negotiate Hajj quotas for Nigeria (95,000+ pilgrims)
(f) Provide Hajj orientation and religious education for pilgrims
(g) Regulate Hajj tour operators and travel agents
(h) License Hajj operators ensuring compliance
(i) Airlift pilgrims to Saudi Arabia and return
(j) Provide accommodation in Mecca, Medina and Mina

(o) Regulate Hajj costs and packages preventing exploitation

## 709. NIGERIA CHRISTIAN PILGRIMS COMMISSION (NCPC)

**MDA Code:** 161015001

Nigeria Christian Pilgrims Commission is federal agency organizing and regulating Christian pilgrimage to Holy Land. Operating under Office of President. Established to coordinate Nigerian Christians' pilgrimage to Jerusalem, Rome and other holy sites. Organizes annual Christian pilgrimage to Israel/Holy Land. Coordinates pilgrimages to Rome, Vatican and other Christian sites. Registers Christian pilgrims from denominations nationwide. Negotiates with Israeli authorities on pilgrimage arrangements. Airlifts pilgrims to Israel and back. Provides accommodation and welfare services in Jerusalem. Regulates Christian tour operators and pilgrimage agents. Conducts pre-departure orientation for pilgrims. Ensures pilgrims' safety and security abroad. Regulates pilgrimage costs preventing exploitation. Collaborates with Israeli tourism authorities. Addresses challenges from high costs, limited quotas, interfaith sensitivities (parallel to NAHCON for Muslims) and pilgrim welfare concerns. Thousands of Nigerian Christians perform Holy Land pilgrimage annually. Pilgrimage represents religious devotion, tourism and international relations. Critical for religious fulfillment, pilgrim protection and managing Christian pilgrimage operations.

**Core Mandate:** To organize Christian pilgrimage to Holy Land, coordinate with Israel, register pilgrims, ensure pilgrim welfare and manage Nigerian Christians' religious pilgrimage.

**Statutory Functions:**
(a) Organize annual Christian pilgrimage to Israel/Holy Land
(b) Coordinate pilgrimages to Rome, Vatican and Christian holy sites
(c) Register Christian pilgrims from denominations nationwide
(d) Negotiate with Israeli authorities on pilgrimage arrangements
(e) Process pilgrimage documentation and Israeli visas
(f) Airlift pilgrims to Israel and return to Nigeria
(g) Provide accommodation in Jerusalem and holy sites
(h) Ensure pilgrims' welfare and safety abroad
(i) Regulate Christian tour operators and pilgrimage agents
(j) License Christian pilgrimage operators
(k) Conduct pre-departure orientation for pilgrims
(l) Regulate pilgrimage costs and packages
(m) Prevent exploitation of pilgrims by operators
(n) Coordinate with Nigerian embassy in Israel
(o) Collaborate with Israeli tourism authorities
(p) Manage pilgrims at Christian holy sites
(q) Address pilgrim complaints and disputes
(r) Promote interfaith harmony with Muslim pilgrimage
(s) Support spiritual and religious fulfillment
(t) Advance religious pilgrimage managing thousands of Nigerian Christians visiting Holy Land ensuring safety, affordability and spiritual fulfillment

# NIGERIAN FEDERAL AGENCY MANDATES: AGENCIES 821-840

## 821. PRESIDENTIAL TECHNICAL COMMITTEE ON LAND REFORMS

**MDA Code:** 161019001

Presidential Technical Committee on Land Reforms is expert panel advising President on land tenure, land administration and land reform policies. Operates under Office of President. Addresses Nigeria's land ownership system including Land Use Act 1978 which vests all land in state governors. Reviews multiple land tenure systems including statutory, customary and religious frameworks. Examines land title registration, Certificates of Occupancy issuance and cadastral survey systems.

**Core Mandate:** To review land laws and policies, recommend land tenure reforms, address land administration challenges and support secure land rights.

**Statutory Functions:**
(a) Review Nigerian land laws and land tenure systems
(b) Assess Land Use Act 1978 implementation and impacts
(c) Recommend land tenure reforms and policy changes
(d) Address land administration inefficiencies and corruption
(e) Propose solutions to land title registration challenges
(f) Review multiple land tenure systems (statutory, customary, religious)
(g) Address conflicts between statutory and customary land rights
(h) Recommend agricultural land access improvements
(i) Address urban land speculation and housing affordability
(j) Propose solutions to land grabbing and fake titles
(k) Review Certificates of Occupancy issuance processes
(l) Address boundary disputes and land conflicts
(m) Recommend cadastral survey and mapping improvements
(n) Propose land information systems and digitalization
(o) Address governor control over land transactions
(p) Review compensation frameworks for land acquisition
(q) Propose women's land rights protections
(r) Review community land rights and indigenous peoples
(s) Recommend investment climate improvements through secure land rights
(t) Advance land reform through comprehensive policy recommendations addressing Land Use Act and land administration systems

## 598. NATIONAL BOUNDARY COMMISSION (NBC)

**MDA Code:** 161021001

National Boundary Commission is federal agency managing Nigeria's international and interstate boundaries. Headquartered in Abuja, operating under Office of the Secretary to Government of Federation. Established to delimit, demarcate and maintain Nigeria's boundaries preventing conflicts. Manages Nigeria's international boundaries with Benin, Niger, Chad and Cameroon spanning approximately 4,900 km. Delimits interstate boundaries between Nigeria's 36 states and FCT preventing boundary disputes. Conducts boundary demarcation and pillaring marking territorial limits. Resolves boundary disputes through arbitration and mediation. Manages Bakassi Peninsula handover to Cameroon following ICJ ruling. Conducts boundary surveys using GPS and mapping technologies. Maintains boundary documentation and maps. Addresses community conflicts arising from boundary uncertainties. Collaborates with neighboring countries on transboundary management. Prevents illegal boundary alterations and encroachments. Addresses challenges from porous borders, boundary-related community conflicts and limited resources for comprehensive demarcation. Critical for territorial integrity, preventing interstate conflicts and managing international relations with neighbors.

**Core Mandate:** To delimit and demarcate Nigeria's boundaries, resolve boundary disputes, maintain territorial integrity and manage international and interstate boundary relations.

**Statutory Functions:**
(a) Manage Nigeria's international boundaries with Benin, Niger, Chad and Cameroon
(b) Delimit interstate boundaries between 36 states and FCT
(c) Conduct boundary demarcation and pillaring operations
(d) Resolve interstate boundary disputes through arbitration
(e) Address community conflicts from boundary uncertainties
(f) Manage Bakassi Peninsula handover implementation
(g) Conduct boundary surveys using modern technology
(h) Maintain boundary documentation, maps and records
(i) Prevent illegal boundary alterations and encroachments
(j) Collaborate with neighboring countries on boundary management
(k) Participate in Joint Boundary Commissions with neighbors
(l) Address porous borders facilitating smuggling and infiltration
(m) Conduct public enlightenment on boundary matters
(n) Mediate boundary disputes between local communities
(o) Prevent creation of unauthorized local governments altering boundaries
(p) Conduct research on boundary issues and conflicts
(q) Coordinate with survey departments on boundary surveys
(r) Engage international boundary organizations
(s) Address maritime boundary delimitation with neighbors
(t) Advance territorial integrity and peaceful boundary management preventing conflicts through professional demarcation and dispute resolution

## 677. NATIONAL QUALITY INFRASTRUCTURE COUNCIL

**MDA Code:** 161024001

National Quality Infrastructure Council is coordinating body for quality assurance, standardization and conformity assessment. Operating under Office of President or Federal Ministry of Industry. Established to coordinate quality infrastructure supporting trade, industry and consumer protection. Coordinates Standards Organisation of Nigeria (SON) on standards development. Oversees conformity assessment and product certification systems. Promotes metrology and measurement standards. Coordinates accreditation bodies ensuring competence. Promotes quality management systems adoption. Supports Made-in-Nigeria products quality improvement. Addresses counterfeit and substandard products. Promotes ISO standards and international quality frameworks. Facilitates export quality requirements compliance. Supports industry competitiveness through quality infrastructure. Collaborates with international quality organizations. Addresses challenges from weak quality culture, counterfeit products flooding markets and limited enforcement. Quality infrastructure critical for trade competitiveness, consumer protection and industrial development. Nigeria's quality infrastructure gaps hinder exports and industrial growth. Critical for quality assurance, standards compliance and supporting competitive manufacturing sector.

**Core Mandate:** To coordinate quality infrastructure, promote standardization, ensure conformity assessment, support quality improvement and advance Nigeria's quality assurance systems supporting trade and industry.

**Statutory Functions:**
(a) Coordinate national quality infrastructure development
(b) Oversee Standards Organisation of Nigeria (SON) coordination
(c) Promote conformity assessment and product certification
(d) Coordinate metrology and measurement standards
(e) Oversee National Metrology Institute operations
(f) Coordinate accreditation bodies ensuring competence
(g) Promote quality management systems (ISO 9001) adoption
(h) Support Made-in-Nigeria products quality improvement
(i) Address counterfeit and substandard products
(j) Promote ISO standards across industries
(k) Facilitate export quality requirements compliance
(l) Support industry competitiveness through quality infrastructure
(m) Coordinate testing laboratories accreditation
(n) Promote certification schemes for products and services
(o) Collaborate with international quality organizations
(p) Harmonize quality standards with regional trading partners
(q) Promote consumer protection through quality assurance
(r) Address quality infrastructure gaps hindering trade
(s) Support manufacturing sector quality improvement
(t) Advance quality assurance systems coordinating standards, metrology and conformity assessment supporting industrial competitiveness and trade despite quality culture weaknesses

## 654. NATIONAL MERIT AWARD

**MDA Code:** 164002001

National Merit Award is federal honors system recognizing exceptional achievements and contributions to Nigeria. Operating under Office of President or Federal Ministry of Special Duties. Established to honor Nigerians excelling in various fields and promoting excellence. Awards National Honors to distinguished Nigerians including Order of the Federal Republic (OFR), Officer of the Order of the Niger (OON), Member of the Order of the Federal Republic (MFR), Member of the Order of the Niger (MON) and Commander of the Order of the Niger (CON). Recognizes achievements in public service, business, arts, sports, academia and philanthropy. Celebrates October 1st Independence Day with honors announcements. Promotes national pride and excellence. Recognizes contributions to nation building and development. Honors military service, bravery and sacrifice. Awards posthumous honors to deceased heroes. Collaborates with honors committees selecting recipients. Addresses controversies - some awardees perceived as undeserving, political considerations affecting selections. National honors sometimes devalued by perceived political patronage. Critical for recognizing excellence, promoting role models and celebrating Nigerians' achievements across all sectors inspiring others to excellence.

**Core Mandate:** To administer national honors system, recognize exceptional achievements, award national honors, promote excellence and celebrate Nigerians' contributions to national development.

**Statutory Functions:**
(a) Administer Nigeria's national honors and merit award system
(b) Award Order of the Federal Republic (OFR) to distinguished Nigerians
(c) Award Officer of the Order of the Niger (OON) for exceptional service
(d) Award Member of the Order of the Federal Republic (MFR)
(e) Award Member of the Order of the Niger (MON)
(f) Award Commander of the Order of the Niger (CON)
(g) Award Grand Commander of the Order of the Niger (GCON)
(h) Recognize achievements in public service and governance
(i) Honor excellence in business and entrepreneurship
(j) Recognize contributions to arts, culture and entertainment
(k) Honor achievements in sports and athletics
(l) Recognize academic excellence and scholarly contributions
(m) Honor philanthropic work and humanitarian service
(n) Celebrate October 1st Independence with honors announcements
(o) Promote national pride and excellence
(p) Recognize contributions to nation building and development
(q) Honor military service, bravery and sacrifice
(r) Award posthumous honors to deceased national heroes
(s) Collaborate with honors selection committees
(t) Advance excellence recognition celebrating Nigerians' achievements promoting role models and inspiring excellence across public service, business, arts and all sectors

## 652. NATIONAL LOTTERY TRUST FUND

**MDA Code:** 164003001

National Lottery Trust Fund is federal agency managing lottery proceeds for social development projects. Operating under Federal Ministry of Special Duties. Established to channel lottery revenues into education, health, sports and social welfare projects. Receives percentage of lottery revenues for development. Funds education infrastructure and scholarship programs. Supports healthcare facilities and medical equipment. Finances sports development and athletic facilities. Supports youth and women empowerment programs. Funds community development projects. Provides grants to social welfare organizations. Supports persons with disabilities programs. Finances cultural heritage and arts projects. Promotes poverty alleviation initiatives. Provides funding for disaster relief and emergency response. Supports orphanages and vulnerable children. Ensures transparency in lottery proceeds utilization. Collaborates with beneficiary communities and organizations. Monitors funded projects implementation. Addresses challenges from limited lottery revenues, project sustainability and ensuring funds reach intended beneficiaries. Lottery Trust Fund represents lottery's social responsibility - gaming revenues supporting development. Critical for social development, community projects and ensuring lottery benefits public good.

**Core Mandate:** To manage lottery proceeds for development, fund education and health projects, support social welfare, promote sports development and ensure lottery benefits communities.

**Statutory Functions:**

(b) Receive percentage of lottery revenues designated for development

(k) Support programs for persons with disabilities
(l) Finance cultural heritage preservation and arts projects
(m) Promote poverty alleviation and livelihood programs
(n) Provide funding for disaster relief and emergency assistance
(o) Support orphanages and vulnerable children programs
(p) Fund elderly care and senior citizens welfare
(q) Ensure transparency in lottery proceeds utilization
(r) Collaborate with beneficiary communities on project selection
(s) Monitor implementation of funded projects
(t) Advance social development ensuring lottery revenues benefit public good through education, health, sports and community projects demonstrating gaming industry's social responsibility

## 651. NATIONAL LOTTERY REGULATORY COMMISSION

**MDA Code:** 164004001

National Lottery Regulatory Commission is federal agency regulating lottery and gaming industry. Operating under Federal Ministry of Special Duties. Established to license, regulate and supervise lottery operations ensuring transparency and protecting players. Licenses lottery operators and gaming companies. Regulates lottery games, draws and prize payments. Ensures fair and transparent lottery operations. Protects lottery players from fraud and manipulation. Monitors lottery draws and results verification. Regulates online lottery and digital gaming platforms. Issues lottery operating licenses to qualified companies. Enforces lottery regulations and compliance. Addresses illegal lottery operations and unlicensed operators. Ensures lottery proceeds contribute to social development. Promotes responsible gaming and addiction prevention. Collaborates with security agencies on lottery fraud. Addresses challenges from lottery scams defrauding Nigerians, illegal operators and inadequate enforcement. Lottery industry generates significant revenues but faces integrity concerns. Sports betting and lottery extremely popular in Nigeria. Critical for gaming regulation, consumer protection and ensuring lottery industry transparency preventing fraud while supporting legitimate gaming operations.

**Core Mandate:** To regulate lottery and gaming industry, license operators, ensure transparent operations, protect players and prevent lottery fraud and illegal gaming.

**Statutory Functions:**
(a) Regulate lottery and gaming industry in Nigeria
(b) License lottery operators and gaming companies
(c) Regulate lottery games, draws and prize structures
(d) Ensure fair and transparent lottery operations
(e) Protect lottery players from fraud and manipulation
(f) Monitor lottery draws and results verification

(h) Issue lottery operating licenses to qualified applicants
(i) Enforce lottery regulations and industry compliance
(j) Address illegal lottery operations and unlicensed gaming
(k) Ensure lottery prize payments to winners
(l) Monitor lottery advertising and marketing
(m) Ensure lottery proceeds benefit social development
(n) Promote responsible gaming and gambling addiction prevention
(o) Address lottery scams defrauding Nigerians
(p) Collaborate with security agencies on lottery fraud
(q) Regulate sports betting and gaming platforms
(r) Monitor online gaming and prevent underage gambling
(s) Promote transparency in gaming industry
(t) Advance gaming regulation and consumer protection ensuring lottery transparency preventing fraud while supporting legitimate gaming operations in growing Nigerian lottery and betting industry

# NIGERIAN FEDERAL AGENCY MANDATES: AGENCIES 602-621

## 602. NATIONAL CENTRE FOR AGRICULTURAL MECHANISATION (NCAM), ILORIN

**MDA Code:** 215004001

National Centre for Agricultural Mechanisation is federal agricultural research institution developing agricultural machinery, mechanization technologies and farm equipment. Located in Ilorin, Kwara State in north-central Nigeria, operating under Agricultural Research Council of Nigeria and Federal Ministry of Agriculture. Established to promote agricultural mechanization reducing manual labor and increasing productivity. Develops tractors, implements and farm machinery adapted to Nigerian conditions. Researches appropriate mechanization technologies for smallholder farmers. Designs and fabricates tillage equipment, planters and harvesters. Develops processing equipment for cassava, rice, maize and other crops. Researches mechanization for different agroecological zones. Provides training on operation and maintenance of farm machinery. Fabricates prototypes of agricultural equipment. Conducts demonstrations showcasing mechanization benefits. Collaborates with tractor assembly plants and equipment manufacturers. Addresses challenges from high equipment costs, limited adoption by smallholders and maintenance difficulties. Nigeria's low mechanization (0.27 HP/ha vs. recommended 1.5 HP/ha) constrains productivity. Critical for agricultural transformation, reducing drudgery and increasing farm productivity.

**Core Mandate:** To develop agricultural machinery, promote mechanization technologies, train farmers and technicians, and advance agricultural mechanization reducing manual labor and increasing productivity.

**Statutory Functions:**
(a) Develop agricultural machinery and farm equipment for Nigerian agriculture
(b) Research appropriate mechanization technologies for smallholder farmers
(c) Design and fabricate tractors adapted to tropical conditions
(d) Develop tillage equipment including ploughs, harrows and cultivators
(e) Design planters, seeders and crop establishment equipment
(f) Develop harvesters and harvesting technologies
(g) Design processing equipment for cassava, rice and maize
(h) Develop threshing and winnowing machines
(i) Research mechanization for different agroecological zones
(j) Adapt mechanization to small farm sizes
(k) Develop power tillers for small-scale farmers
(l) Design irrigation pumps and water management equipment
(m) Provide training on operation of farm machinery
(n) Train technicians on maintenance and repair of equipment
(o) Fabricate prototypes and pilot equipment
(p) Conduct field demonstrations showcasing mechanization
(q) Collaborate with tractor manufacturers and equipment importers
(r) Promote affordable mechanization technologies
(s) Address Nigeria's low mechanization level (0.27 HP/ha)
(t) Advance agricultural mechanization and farm productivity reducing manual drudgery, increasing efficiency and supporting agricultural transformation

---

## 606. NATIONAL CEREALS RESEARCH INSTITUTE (NCRI), BADEGGI

**MDA Code:** 215005001

National Cereals Research Institute is federal agricultural research institution conducting research on cereal crops including rice, sorghum, maize, millet and wheat. Located in Badeggi, Niger State in north-central Nigeria, operating under Agricultural Research Council of Nigeria and Federal Ministry of Agriculture. Established to develop improved cereal varieties, production technologies and address Nigeria's cereal production challenges. Conducts rice research developing high-yielding varieties adapted to upland, lowland and irrigated conditions. Researches sorghum and millet for semi-arid regions. Develops improved maize varieties including drought-tolerant and early-maturing types. Researches wheat production for northern Nigeria. Develops integrated pest and disease management for cereals. Researches soil fertility and crop nutrition. Conducts post-harvest and processing research. Multiplies and distributes improved seeds to farmers. Provides training for farmers and extension agents. Collaborates with IITA, IRRI and international agricultural research centers. Addresses food security - cereals provide staple food for majority of Nigerians. Nigeria imports rice worth billions annually despite production potential. Critical for food self-sufficiency and reducing cereal imports.

**Core Mandate:** To conduct cereal crop research, develop improved varieties, generate production technologies and support rice, maize, sorghum, millet and wheat production for food security.

**Statutory Functions:**
(a) Conduct research on cereal crops (rice, maize, sorghum, millet, wheat)
(b) Develop high-yielding rice varieties for upland, lowland and irrigated systems
(c) Research sorghum and millet for semi-arid northern regions
(d) Develop improved maize varieties including hybrids
(e) Research drought-tolerant and climate-resilient cereal varieties
(f) Develop early-maturing varieties for short growing seasons
(g) Research wheat production for northern Nigeria
(h) Develop integrated pest management for cereal pests
(i) Research disease-resistant varieties (blast, smut, striga)
(j) Conduct soil fertility and nutrient management research
(k) Research post-harvest handling and storage technologies
(l) Develop cereal processing and value addition technologies
(m) Multiply and distribute improved cereal seeds
(n) Conduct adaptive research across agroecological zones
(o) Provide training for farmers on cereal production
(p) Train agricultural extension agents on technologies
(q) Operate research farms and demonstration plots
(r) Collaborate with IITA and international rice research institutes
(s) Address Nigeria's rice import dependency (billions spent annually)
(t) Advance food security and cereal self-sufficiency through research supporting domestic rice, maize and sorghum production reducing import dependence

---

## 679. NATIONAL ROOT CROPS RESEARCH INSTITUTE (NRCRI), UMUDIKE

**MDA Code:** 215007001

National Root Crops Research Institute is federal agricultural research institution conducting research on root and tuber crops. Located in Umudike, Abia State in southeastern Nigeria, operating under Agricultural Research Council of Nigeria and Federal Ministry of Agriculture. Established to develop improved varieties and production technologies for cassava, yam, sweet potato, cocoyam and other root crops. Conducts cassava research developing high-yielding disease-resistant varieties. Researches yam production, storage and processing technologies. Develops sweet potato varieties with improved nutrition. Conducts cocoyam research addressing taro leaf blight. Researches ginger, turmeric and other root crops. Develops virus-resistant and pest-resistant root crop varieties. Researches root crop processing and value addition. Develops improved storage technologies reducing post-harvest losses. Multiplies and distributes improved planting materials. Collaborates with IITA on cassava and root crops research. Root crops critical for food security - cassava staple for millions, yam culturally important. Nigeria world's largest cassava producer but yields low. Cassava for industrial starch, ethanol, animal feed. Critical for food security, industrial raw materials and root crops sector development.

**Core Mandate:** To conduct root crops research, develop improved varieties, generate production technologies, support cassava and yam production and advance root crops sector for food security.

**Statutory Functions:**
(a) Conduct research on root and tuber crops (cassava, yam, sweet potato, cocoyam)
(b) Develop high-yielding cassava varieties for food and industry
(c) Research disease-resistant cassava (cassava mosaic disease, bacterial blight)
(d) Develop yam varieties with improved yields and storage quality
(e) Research sweet potato varieties with enhanced nutrition (orange-fleshed)
(f) Conduct cocoyam research addressing taro leaf blight
(g) Research ginger, turmeric and medicinal root crops
(h) Develop virus-resistant and pest-resistant varieties
(i) Research root crop processing technologies
(j) Develop cassava processing for starch, flour and ethanol
(k) Research yam storage technologies reducing losses
(l) Develop value addition and industrial applications
(m) Multiply and distribute improved planting materials
(n) Research sustainable root crop production systems
(o) Conduct agronomic research on root crops management
(p) Train farmers on improved root crops production
(q) Collaborate with IITA on cassava improvement
(r) Address Nigeria's cassava industrial utilization
(s) Research climate-smart root crop varieties
(t) Advance food security through root crops research supporting cassava (world's largest producer) and yam production providing industrial raw materials and staple foods despite low yields requiring improvement

## 644. NATIONAL INSTITUTE FOR OIL PALM RESEARCH (NIFOR), BENIN

**MDA Code:** 215008001

National Institute for Oil Palm Research is premier federal agricultural research institution conducting oil palm research. Located in Benin City, Edo State (major oil palm region), operating under Agricultural Research Council of Nigeria and Federal Ministry of Agriculture. Established to develop improved oil palm varieties and production technologies. Nigeria was world's largest palm oil producer until 1960s, now imports despite potential. Conducts oil palm breeding developing high-yielding varieties with improved oil content. Researches disease and pest management including fusarium wilt. Develops sustainable oil palm plantation management. Researches smallholder oil palm production systems. Conducts processing research on palm oil extraction and quality. Researches palm kernel oil and palm kernel cake utilization. Develops nursery management and seedling production technologies. Multiplies and distributes improved oil palm seedlings. Provides training for oil palm farmers and plantation managers. Collaborates with Malaysian palm oil research institutes. Nigeria imports palm oil worth billions annually despite being original source - Indonesian and Malaysian industries built from Nigerian germplasm. Critical for reviving oil palm sector, import substitution and restoring Nigeria's historical palm oil leadership.

**Core Mandate:** To conduct oil palm research, develop improved varieties, generate production technologies, support oil palm farming and revive Nigeria's palm oil industry reducing import dependence.

**Statutory Functions:**
(a) Conduct oil palm breeding and genetic improvement research
(b) Develop high-yielding oil palm varieties with improved oil content
(c) Research disease-resistant palm varieties (fusarium wilt, bud rot)
(d) Develop pest management strategies for oil palm
(e) Research sustainable oil palm plantation management
(f) Develop smallholder oil palm production technologies
(g) Research oil palm agronomy and cultural practices
(h) Conduct soil management research for oil palm cultivation
(i) Research palm oil processing and extraction technologies
(j) Develop palm oil quality improvement methods
(k) Research palm kernel oil and palm kernel cake utilization
(l) Develop oil palm nursery management technologies
(m) Multiply and distribute improved oil palm seedlings to farmers
(n) Provide training for oil palm farmers and plantation workers
(o) Train plantation managers on best practices
(p) Conduct post-harvest and palm oil storage research
(q) Research climate-smart oil palm production
(r) Collaborate with Malaysian and Indonesian palm oil institutes
(s) Address Nigeria's palm oil import dependence (billions annually)
(t) Advance oil palm sector revival and import substitution restoring Nigeria's historical palm oil leadership through research addressing production gaps despite being original global palm oil source

---

## 630. NATIONAL HORTICULTURAL RESEARCH INSTITUTE (NIHORT), IBADAN

**MDA Code:** 215011001

National Horticultural Research Institute is federal agricultural research institution conducting research on fruits, vegetables and ornamental plants. Located in Ibadan, Oyo State, operating under Agricultural Research Council of Nigeria and Federal Ministry of Agriculture. Established to develop improved horticultural varieties, production technologies and post-harvest systems. Conducts research on vegetables including tomatoes, peppers, okra, leafy vegetables and cucurbits. Researches fruit crops including citrus, mango, pineapple, banana, plantain and pawpaw. Develops improved varieties with higher yields, disease resistance and better quality. Researches ornamental plants, flowers and landscaping. Develops post-harvest handling and storage technologies reducing losses. Researches vegetable processing and preservation. Conducts nursery management and seedling production research. Develops greenhouse and protected cultivation technologies. Researches urban agriculture and home gardening. Multiplies and distributes improved planting materials. Collaborates with IITA and international horticultural organizations. Addresses food security - vegetables provide nutrition and micronutrients. Post-harvest losses estimated 40-50% in perishable vegetables. Critical for nutrition security, vegetable production and reducing post-harvest losses.

**Core Mandate:** To conduct horticultural research, develop improved fruit and vegetable varieties, reduce post-harvest losses and support vegetable production for nutrition security.

**Statutory Functions:**
(a) Conduct research on vegetables and vegetable production
(b) Develop improved tomato varieties resistant to diseases
(c) Research pepper production including sweet and hot peppers
(d) Develop improved okra, leafy vegetables and cucurbits
(e) Conduct fruit crop research on citrus, mango and tropical fruits
(f) Research banana, plantain and pineapple production
(g) Develop improved horticultural varieties with high yields
(h) Research disease and pest resistance in vegetables
(i) Develop post-harvest handling technologies
(j) Research storage systems reducing vegetable losses (40-50%)
(k) Conduct vegetable processing and preservation research
(l) Research nursery management and quality seedling production
(m) Develop greenhouse and protected cultivation technologies
(n) Research urban agriculture and home vegetable gardens
(o) Develop irrigation technologies for vegetable production
(p) Multiply and distribute improved planting materials
(q) Conduct ornamental plant and floriculture research
(r) Provide training for vegetable farmers
(s) Collaborate with IITA on horticultural research
(t) Advance nutrition security and vegetable production through research reducing massive post-harvest losses and improving fruit and vegetable availability

Rubber Research Institute of Nigeria is federal agricultural research institution for rubber production. Located in Benin City, Edo State. Operates under Federal Ministry of Agriculture. ## 585. NATIONAL AGRICULTURAL EXTENSION AND RESEARCH LIAISON SERVICES (NAERLS), ZARIA

**MDA Code:** 215018001

National Agricultural Extension and Research Liaison Services (NAERLS) is federal agency coordinating agricultural extension, research-farmer linkages and agricultural information dissemination. Located at Ahmadu Bello University, Zaria, Kaduna State, operating under Federal Ministry of Agriculture. Established to bridge gap between agricultural research institutions and farmers ensuring research findings reach farming communities. Coordinates Agricultural Development Programs (ADPs) extension services across states. Conducts agricultural training for extension agents. Provides technical backstopping for ADPs. Disseminates improved agricultural technologies and practices to farmers. Conducts needs assessment identifying farmers' constraints and research priorities. Facilitates technology transfer from research institutes to farmers. Conducts impact assessment of agricultural programs. Provides agricultural statistics and farm surveys. Monitors crop production and agricultural trends. Trains farmers on improved varieties, pest management and farming systems. Collaborates with agricultural research institutes, universities and ADPs. Addresses challenges from weak extension services, limited extension workers and technology adoption gaps. Critical for agricultural productivity, technology uptake and linking research to farmers.

**Core Mandate:** To coordinate agricultural extension services, facilitate research-farmer linkages, disseminate agricultural technologies and ensure research findings benefit farming communities across Nigeria.

(b) Train agricultural extension agents and field workers
(c) Provide technical support and backstopping for state ADPs
(d) Disseminate improved agricultural technologies to farmers
(e) Facilitate technology transfer from research institutes to farming communities
(f) Conduct agricultural training programs for farmers
(g) Conduct needs assessment identifying farmers' constraints
(h) Assess impact of agricultural programs and interventions
(i) Provide agricultural statistics and production data
(j) Conduct farm surveys and agricultural monitoring
(k) Promote improved crop varieties from research institutes
(l) Disseminate integrated pest management technologies
(m) Promote sustainable farming systems and conservation agriculture
(n) Train farmers on post-harvest handling and value addition
(o) Coordinate farmer training and demonstration farms
(p) Publish extension bulletins and agricultural information materials
(q) Collaborate with agricultural research institutes on technology packages
(r) Coordinate with universities on agricultural extension research
(s) Link farmers to agricultural input suppliers and markets
(t) Advance agricultural productivity and technology adoption bridging research-farmer gap ensuring scientific innovations reach smallholder farmers improving food production

## 587. NATIONAL AGRICULTURAL INSURANCE CORPORATION (NAIC)

**MDA Code:** 215035001

National Agricultural Insurance Corporation is federal agency providing crop insurance and agricultural risk management. Headquartered in Abuja, operating under Federal Ministry of Agriculture. Established to protect farmers against crop losses from natural disasters, pests, diseases and climate variability. Operates Nigeria Agricultural Insurance Scheme (NAIS) providing subsidized crop insurance. Insures crops against drought, flood, pests, diseases and other perils. Provides livestock insurance for cattle, poultry and fish farms. Insures agricultural assets including equipment and infrastructure. Offers index-based insurance using weather parameters. Provides forest insurance for tree plantations. Conducts damage assessment and pays compensation to insured farmers. Government provides premium subsidy making insurance affordable. Promotes agricultural insurance awareness among farming communities. Collaborates with state governments, insurance companies and farmers' organizations. Addresses challenges from low insurance penetration (less than 1% farmers insured), delayed claims payment, limited awareness and adverse selection. Critical for risk management, encouraging agricultural investment and protecting farmers' livelihoods from climate shocks.

**Core Mandate:** To provide agricultural insurance, protect farmers against crop losses, manage agricultural risks and promote insurance adoption supporting farming resilience and food security.

**Statutory Functions:**
(a) Operate Nigeria Agricultural Insurance Scheme (NAIS)
(b) Provide crop insurance protecting against natural disasters and pests
(c) Insure crops against drought, flood, hailstorm and fire
(d) Provide pest and disease insurance for crops
(e) Offer livestock insurance for cattle, goats, sheep and pigs
(f) Provide poultry insurance for commercial chicken production
(g) Insure fish farms and aquaculture projects
(h) Provide insurance for agricultural equipment and machinery
(i) Offer infrastructure insurance for storage facilities and processing plants
(j) Develop index-based insurance using rainfall and weather data
(k) Provide forest insurance for timber plantations
(l) Conduct damage assessment and loss verification
(m) Pay compensation to insured farmers suffering losses
(n) Administer government premium subsidy program (50-80% subsidy)
(o) Promote agricultural insurance awareness among farmers
(p) Train insurance agents on agricultural insurance products
(q) Collaborate with state governments on farmer registration
(r) Partner with insurance companies on underwriting
(s) Develop innovative insurance products for smallholder farmers
(t) Advance agricultural risk management and farmer protection increasing insurance penetration supporting climate resilience and encouraging agricultural investment

## 756. NIGERIAN INSTITUTE OF SOIL SCIENCE (NISS)

**MDA Code:** 215037001

Nigerian Institute of Soil Science is professional body regulating soil science profession and promoting soil management. Operating under Federal Ministry of Agriculture. Established to regulate soil scientists, promote sustainable soil management and advance soil science. Regulates soil science profession in Nigeria. Registers soil scientists and soil conservationists. Accredits soil science programs in universities. Promotes soil fertility management and conservation. Addresses soil degradation and erosion. Advances soil testing and analysis. Promotes fertilizer use efficiency and recommendations. Supports precision agriculture and soil mapping. Researches soil-crop relationships. Collaborates with agricultural research on soil management. Addresses challenges from soil degradation, fertility decline, erosion and poor soil management practices. Nigeria's soils degraded from continuous cultivation, erosion and nutrient mining. Soil fertility critical for crop productivity. Critical for soil conservation, fertility management and sustainable agriculture through professional soil science despite degradation challenges.

(e) Address soil degradation and land degradation

(g) Advance soil testing and analysis services
(h) Promote fertilizer use efficiency and application
(i) Provide soil-based fertilizer recommendations
(j) Support precision agriculture and site-specific management
(k) Promote soil mapping and classification
(l) Research soil-crop relationships and productivity
(m) Study soil organic matter and carbon sequestration
(n) Promote soil conservation practices
(o) Address continuous cultivation impacts on fertility
(p) Combat nutrient mining and soil nutrient depletion
(q) Collaborate with agricultural research institutes
(r) Promote integrated soil fertility management
(s) Address poor farmer soil management practices
(t) Advance soil conservation and fertility through professional regulation addressing degradation and declining fertility critical for agricultural productivity

## 729. NIGERIA STORED PRODUCTS RESEARCH INSTITUTE (NSPRI), ILORIN

**MDA Code:** 215050001

Nigeria Stored Products Research Institute is federal research institution studying post-harvest losses and grain storage. Located in Ilorin, Kwara State, operating under Federal Ministry of Agriculture. Established to reduce post-harvest losses through improved storage technologies and pest management. Researches grain storage technologies and systems. Studies storage pests and insect damage to stored products. Develops pest control methods for warehouses and silos. Researches preservation technologies for grains and crops. Studies post-harvest handling reducing losses. Develops storage structures for smallholder farmers. Researches chemical and biological pest control. Studies mycotoxins and aflatoxin contamination in stored grains. Develops hermetic storage technologies. Provides training on post-harvest management. Collaborates with farmers on storage improvement. Addresses massive post-harvest losses - estimated 30-40% grains lost to pests, moisture and poor storage. Nigeria produces grains but losses reduce availability. Storage pest damage causes significant economic losses. Critical for food security, reducing post-harvest losses and ensuring grain availability through improved storage despite enormous current losses.

(j) Research moisture management in stored grains
(k) Study rodent damage and control methods
(l) Develop fumigation techniques for warehouses
(m) Provide training on post-harvest loss reduction
(n) Collaborate with farmers on improved storage practices
(o) Address 30-40% post-harvest losses to pests and poor storage
(p) Promote good storage practices nationwide
(q) Research climate-appropriate storage technologies
(r) Develop storage monitoring systems
(s) Study value chain losses from harvest to consumption
(t) Advance food security through post-harvest loss reduction developing storage technologies addressing massive 30-40% grain losses to pests and poor storage

## 589. NATIONAL AGRICULTURE SEEDS COUNCIL

**MDA Code:** 215051001

National Agriculture Seeds Council is federal regulatory agency overseeing seed industry, variety release and seed certification. Operating under Federal Ministry of Agriculture. Established to regulate seed production, distribution and ensure quality seeds for farmers. Registers and releases new crop varieties for commercial production. Conducts variety evaluation trials assessing performance. Certifies seeds ensuring genetic purity and quality. Licenses seed companies and producers. Regulates seed importation and exportation. Maintains National Crop Varieties and Livestock Breeds Register. Enforces seed quality standards and labeling requirements. Conducts seed testing in laboratories. Promotes use of improved and certified seeds. Protects plant breeders' rights. Coordinates with agricultural research institutes on variety release. Addresses challenges from fake seeds, weak enforcement and limited seed production capacity. Quality seeds critical for agricultural productivity improvements - seed quality determines 30-40% of crop yields. Ensures farmers access genetically pure, disease-free and high-yielding seeds.

## 705. NIGERIA AGRICULTURAL QUARANTINE SERVICE (NAQS)

**MDA Code:** 215053001

Nigeria Agricultural Quarantine Service is federal regulatory agency preventing entry and spread of plant and animal pests and diseases. Operating under Federal Ministry of Agriculture. Established to protect Nigerian agriculture from exotic pests, diseases and invasive species. Conducts quarantine inspections at borders, airports and seaports. Prevents entry of plant pests and diseases from imported crops. Inspects animal imports for diseases. Issues phytosanitary certificates for agricultural exports. Regulates importation of plants, seeds and plant products. Prevents entry of invasive species threatening agriculture. Conducts fumigation and treatment of imported goods. Monitors international pest outbreaks preventing entry to Nigeria. Enforces international phytosanitary standards. Collaborates with customs on agricultural imports inspection. Addresses challenges from porous borders allowing smuggled agricultural products, limited inspection capacity and emerging pest threats. Fall armyworm outbreak demonstrated vulnerability - devastating maize crops. Tomato Ebola virus affected tomato production. Critical for agricultural biosecurity, protecting crops/livestock from foreign pests and ensuring safe agricultural trade.

**Core Mandate:** To prevent entry of agricultural pests and diseases, conduct quarantine inspections, protect Nigerian agriculture, ensure biosecurity and regulate agricultural imports/exports.

(b) Conduct quarantine inspections at borders, airports and seaports
(c) Inspect imported crops, seeds and plant materials
(d) Prevent animal disease entry from imported livestock
(e) Issue phytosanitary certificates for agricultural exports
(f) Regulate importation of plants and plant products
(g) Prevent entry of invasive species threatening agriculture
(h) Conduct fumigation and treatment of infested imports
(i) Monitor international pest and disease outbreaks
(j) Prevent fall armyworm and exotic pest entry
(k) Enforce International Plant Protection Convention standards
(l) Collaborate with customs on agricultural imports
(m) Inspect baggage for prohibited agricultural products
(n) Address smuggled agricultural products through porous borders
(o) Conduct pest risk analysis for new crops
(p) Quarantine infected materials preventing disease spread
(q) Train quarantine inspectors and officers
(r) Provide agricultural biosecurity advisory services
(s) Monitor emerging pest and disease threats
(t) Advance agricultural biosecurity protecting crops and livestock from exotic pests and diseases ensuring safe agricultural trade

Office of the Permanent Representative to FAO is Nigerian diplomatic mission to Food and Agriculture Organization. Located in Rome, Italy, operating under Federal Ministry of Agriculture and Foreign Affairs. Established to represent Nigeria at FAO, coordinate agricultural cooperation and access FAO programs. Represents Nigeria at Food and Agriculture Organization (FAO). Coordinates Nigeria's participation in FAO programs and initiatives. Accesses FAO technical assistance for agriculture. Promotes Nigerian agricultural interests internationally. Participates in FAO governance and policy-making. Coordinates food security and nutrition programs. Accesses FAO support for livestock, fisheries and forestry. Promotes sustainable agriculture and rural development cooperation. Collaborates on agricultural research and technology transfer. **MDA Code:** 215061001

National Agricultural Development Fund is federal financing mechanism providing credit and investment support for agricultural development. Operating under Federal Ministry of Agriculture and Rural Development. Established to provide affordable financing for agricultural projects, agribusinesses and rural development initiatives. Provides credit facilities to farmers, agro-processors and agricultural enterprises. Supports agricultural value chain financing from production to marketing. Finances agricultural infrastructure development. Provides concessional loans for mechanization and irrigation. Supports youth and women in agriculture programs. Finances agricultural research and development projects. Provides guarantees facilitating commercial bank lending to agriculture. Supports climate-smart agriculture and sustainable farming. Finances livestock development and fisheries projects. Collaborates with CBN Anchor Borrowers Program and agricultural financing initiatives. Addresses challenges from loan recovery, limited capital and targeting small-scale farmers. Critical for agricultural financing gap, rural development and supporting farmers' capital needs for productivity improvement.

## 782. OFFICE OF THE ACCOUNTANT-GENERAL OF THE FEDERATION (OAGF)

**MDA Code:** 220007001

Office of the Accountant-General of the Federation is apex federal accounting and financial management office. Operating under Federal Ministry of Finance. Established to manage federal government accounts, ensure financial accountability and coordinate public sector accounting. Manages all federal government accounts and finances. Coordinates revenue collection and government expenditure. Maintains Consolidated Revenue Fund and public accounts. Processes federal government payments and disbursements. Manages payroll for federal civil servants. Implements Treasury Single Account (TSA) policy. Ensures financial accountability and transparency. Prepares federal government financial statements. Coordinates with CBN on government banking. Implements International Public Sector Accounting Standards (IPSAS). Addresses challenges from payroll fraud (ghost workers), pension payment irregularities, weak financial controls and TSA implementation resistance. TSA consolidated fragmented government accounts into single CBN account (2015). Addresses billions lost to financial leakages. Critical for public financial management, accountability and ensuring proper use of government revenues despite fraud and control weaknesses.

**Core Mandate:** To manage federal government accounts, ensure financial accountability, coordinate revenue and expenditure, implement TSA and maintain public financial management systems.

**Statutory Functions:**
(a) Manage all federal government accounts and public finances
(b) Coordinate federal government revenue collection
(c) Manage government expenditure and disbursements
(d) Maintain Consolidated Revenue Fund accounts
(e) Process federal government payments nationwide
(f) Manage payroll for federal civil servants
(g) Implement Treasury Single Account (TSA) policy
(h) Ensure financial accountability and transparency
(i) Prepare federal government financial statements
(j) Coordinate with Central Bank on government banking
(k) Implement International Public Sector Accounting Standards (IPSAS)
(l) Combat payroll fraud and ghost workers
(m) Address pension payment irregularities
(n) Strengthen financial controls and internal audit
(o) Address TSA implementation resistance from agencies
(p) Prevent revenue leakages and financial losses
(q) Manage government cash flow and liquidity
(r) Coordinate with revenue agencies (FIRS, Customs, NNPC)
(s) Implement financial management reforms
(t) Advance public financial management ensuring accountability preventing fraud (ghost workers, leakages) through TSA and controls

## 794. PENSION TRANSITIONAL ARRANGEMENT DIRECTORATE (PTAD)

**MDA Code:** 220015001

Pension Transitional Arrangement Directorate is federal agency managing pensions for pre-2004 retirees under old pension scheme. Operating under Federal Ministry of Finance. Established to pay pensions for retirees who retired before Contributory Pension Scheme (2004). Manages pensions for pre-2004 federal government retirees. Pays monthly pensions under old "pay-as-you-go" defined benefit scheme. Processes pension and gratuity payments. Conducts biometric verification preventing pension fraud. Addresses pension arrears and outstanding payments. Manages pensioners database for old scheme retirees. Collaborates with PenCom on pension coordination. Processes death benefits for deceased pensioners' families. Addresses ghost pensioners and payroll fraud. Conducts physical verification exercises. Pays military, police, civil service and parastatal pensions. Faces challenges from massive pension arrears (billions owed), limited funding, ghost pensioners and verification difficulties. Old pension scheme unfunded creating huge liabilities. Many elderly pensioners suffer from non-payment. Critical for retiree welfare, pension payments and managing old scheme obligations despite arrears and fraud challenges.

**Core Mandate:** To manage pensions for pre-2004 retirees, pay old scheme pensions, address pension arrears, prevent fraud and ensure retiree welfare under defined benefit system.

**Statutory Functions:**
(a) Manage pensions for pre-2004 federal government retirees
(b) Pay monthly pensions under old "pay-as-you-go" scheme
(c) Process pension and gratuity payments for retirees
(d) Conduct biometric verification preventing pension fraud
(e) Address massive pension arrears owed to retirees
(f) Manage pensioners database for old scheme beneficiaries
(g) Collaborate with PenCom on pension sector coordination
(h) Process death benefits for deceased pensioners' families
(i) Combat ghost pensioners and payroll fraud
(j) Conduct physical verification exercises for pensioners
(k) Pay military retirees' pensions from old scheme
(l) Process police retirees' pension payments
(12) Manage civil service retirees' pensions
(n) Pay parastatals and agencies retirees
(o) Address limited funding for pension payments
(p) Tackle billions in outstanding pension arrears
(q) Remove ghost pensioners from payroll
(r) Improve verification processes and systems
(s) Support elderly pensioners suffering from non-payment
(t) Advance retiree welfare managing old scheme pensions despite massive arrears, ghost pensioners and unfunded liabilities

## 738. NIGERIAN BULK ELECTRICITY TRADING PLC (NBET)

**MDA Code:** 220023001

Nigerian Bulk Electricity Trading PLC is federal power sector agency managing electricity trading and payment assurance. Operating under Federal Ministry of Power. Established post-privatization to buy electricity from Generation Companies (GenCos) and sell to Distribution Companies (DisCos). Purchases electricity from power generation companies. Sells electricity to distribution companies. Provides payment assurance to GenCos encouraging generation. Manages power purchase agreements. Coordinates electricity market transactions. Addresses payment failures in power sector value chain. Mitigates financial risks for power generators. Implements transitional electricity market arrangements. Promotes market liquidity in power sector. Addresses DisCos' payment defaults to GenCos. Funded partially by government to ensure GenCos payments despite DisCos non-payment. Faces massive challenges - DisCos collect tariffs but don't pay fully, circular debt in power sector, government subsidizing payments, financial sustainability concerns. NBET prevents sector collapse from payment failures. Critical for power sector financial stability, ensuring generators receive payment and preventing electricity market breakdown despite enormous circular debt.

**Core Mandate:** To trade bulk electricity, provide payment assurance to generators, manage power purchase agreements and ensure power sector financial stability despite payment challenges.

**Statutory Functions:**
(a) Purchase bulk electricity from Generation Companies (GenCos)
(b) Sell electricity to Distribution Companies (DisCos)
(c) Provide payment assurance to power generators
(d) Manage power purchase agreements between GenCos and off-takers
(e) Coordinate electricity market transactions
(f) Address payment failures in power sector value chain
(g) Mitigate financial risks for electricity generators
(h) Implement transitional electricity market arrangements
(i) Promote market liquidity in power sector
(j) Address DisCos' payment defaults to generation companies
(k) Receive government funding ensuring GenCos payments
(l) Manage circular debt in electricity sector
(m) Prevent sector collapse from payment breakdowns
(n) Coordinate with GenCos on generation contracts
(o) Work with DisCos on off-take obligations
(p) Collaborate with Central Bank on power sector financing
(q) Address tariff collection vs. payment gaps
(r) Promote cost-reflective electricity pricing
(s) Support power sector privatization sustainability
(t) Advance power sector financial stability preventing market collapse through payment assurance despite DisCos defaults and massive circular debt threatening sector viability
