## 592. NATIONAL AUTOMOTIVE DESIGN AND DEVELOPMENT COUNCIL (NADDC)

**MDA Code:** 222004001

National Automotive Design and Development Council is federal agency promoting automotive industry development and local vehicle manufacturing. Headquartered in Abuja, operating under Federal Ministry of Industry, Trade and Investment. Established to develop indigenous automotive design, manufacturing and assembly capacity. Implements National Automotive Industry Development Plan promoting local content. Provides incentives for vehicle assembly plants in Nigeria. Regulates automotive industry and vehicle importation. ## 745. NIGERIAN EXPORT PROMOTION COUNCIL (NEPC)

**MDA Code:** 222006001

Nigerian Export Promotion Council is federal agency promoting non-oil exports and export development. Operating under Federal Ministry of Industry, Trade and Investment. Established to diversify economy through export promotion, develop export capacity and reduce oil dependence. Promotes non-oil exports and export diversification. Supports agricultural exports (cocoa, cashew, sesame, ginger). Promotes manufactured exports and value-added products. Provides export market intelligence and information. Organizes trade fairs and export exhibitions. Facilitates export financing and credit. Trains exporters on export procedures and documentation. Provides export advisory and consultancy services. Promotes "Export for Survival" and "Zero Oil Plan" campaigns. Addresses oil dependence - oil provides 80%+ export earnings. Supports SME export development. Collaborates with NEXIM Bank on export financing. Faces challenges from poor product quality, limited export infrastructure, high logistics costs and competition from other countries. Nigeria's non-oil exports minimal despite potential. Critical for economic diversification, non-oil export development and reducing petroleum dependence through export promotion.

## 744. NIGERIAN EXPORT PROCESSING ZONES AUTHORITY (NEPZA)

**MDA Code:** 222008001

Nigerian Export Processing Zones Authority is federal agency establishing and managing export processing zones for manufacturing and exports. Operating under Federal Ministry of Industry, Trade and Investment. Established to promote export-oriented manufacturing, attract foreign investment and create employment through export zones. Establishes and manages Export Processing Zones (EPZs) nationwide. Promotes export-oriented manufacturing and industries. Attracts foreign direct investment to zones. Provides tax incentives and duty-free imports for zone companies. Creates employment through zone operations. Operates free trade zones including Calabar, Kano, Lekki, Onne. Promotes value addition and manufacturing for exports. Provides infrastructure in export zones. Supports Made-in-Nigeria exports to international markets. Addresses challenges from limited zone development, infrastructure gaps, competing with other countries' zones and attracting sufficient investors. Export zones underutilized despite potential. Import-dependent economy requires export manufacturing. Critical for export promotion, foreign investment attraction and manufacturing development through export processing zones despite underutilization.

**Core Mandate:** To establish export processing zones, promote export manufacturing, attract foreign investment, provide tax incentives and support export-oriented industrialization.

**Statutory Functions:**
(a) Establish and manage Export Processing Zones (EPZs) nationwide
(b) Promote export-oriented manufacturing and industries

## 790. OIL AND GAS FREE ZONES AUTHORITY (OGFZA)

**MDA Code:** 222013001

Oil and Gas Free Zones Authority is federal agency managing specialized free zones for petroleum sector. Operating under Federal Ministry of Petroleum Resources. Established to develop oil and gas free zones, attract investments and support petroleum industry development. Manages oil and gas free trade zones nationwide. Operates Onne Oil and Gas Free Zone, Rivers State (largest). Provides specialized infrastructure for oil/gas companies. Attracts foreign investment to petroleum sector zones. Offers tax incentives and duty-free imports. Supports local content development in oil/gas sector. Provides fabrication and manufacturing facilities. Supports oil services and logistics companies. Develops zones in Brass (Bayelsa), Olokola (Ogun/Ondo). Promotes value addition in petroleum sector. Collaborates with oil majors and service companies. Addresses challenges from limited zone development, local content implementation gaps and competing with foreign facilities. Oil/gas zones support offshore operations. Local fabrication reduces costs. Critical for petroleum sector support, local content promotion and oil/gas industry development through specialized zones.

**Core Mandate:** To manage oil and gas free zones, attract petroleum sector investment, support local content, provide specialized infrastructure and advance oil/gas industry development.

**Statutory Functions:**
(a) Manage oil and gas free trade zones nationwide
(b) Operate Onne Oil and Gas Free Zone, Rivers State
(c) Develop Brass LNG Free Zone, Bayelsa State
(d) Manage Olokola Free Trade Zone, Ogun/Ondo States
(e) Provide specialized infrastructure for oil and gas companies
(f) Attract foreign direct investment to petroleum sector
(g) Offer tax incentives and duty-free imports for zone companies
(h) Support Nigerian Content Development Act implementation
(i) Promote local fabrication and manufacturing
(j) Provide oil services and logistics facilities
(k) Support offshore oil operations with onshore support bases
(l) Develop fabrication yards for platforms and structures
(m) Provide warehousing and supply chain facilities
(n) Support maritime and subsea services
(o) Promote value addition in petroleum sector
(p) Collaborate with Shell, Chevron, Total and oil majors
(q) Address limited zone development and infrastructure
(r) Implement local content requirements for employment
(s) Compete with foreign fabrication facilities abroad
(t) Advance petroleum sector development through specialized zones supporting local content and oil/gas operations

## 758. NIGERIAN INVESTMENT PROMOTION COMMISSION (NIPC)

**MDA Code:** 222030001

Nigerian Investment Promotion Commission is federal agency attracting foreign investment and promoting investment climate. Headquartered in Abuja, operating under Federal Ministry of Industry, Trade and Investment. Established to attract foreign direct investment, facilitate investment and improve Nigeria's investment environment. Promotes foreign direct investment (FDI) to Nigeria. Facilitates investment approvals and permits. Provides investment information and advisory services. Promotes Nigeria as investment destination internationally. Addresses investment barriers and impediments. Coordinates One-Stop Investment Centre (OSIC). Issues business permits and expatriate quotas. Supports investment protection and guarantees. Promotes sectors for investment - agriculture, manufacturing, technology. Collaborates with states on investment promotion. Addresses challenges from poor infrastructure, insecurity, multiple taxation, corruption and regulatory burdens deterring investors. Nigeria attracted $2B FDI (2022) vs. smaller economies attracting more. Investment climate poor. Critical for FDI attraction, investment facilitation and improving investment climate despite significant deterrents.

**Core Mandate:** To attract foreign direct investment, facilitate investment approvals, promote investment climate improvement, support investors and advance Nigeria as investment destination.

**Statutory Functions:**
(a) Promote foreign direct investment (FDI) to Nigeria
(b) Attract international investors and multinational companies
(c) Facilitate investment approvals and regulatory permits
(d) Provide investment information and market intelligence
(e) Offer investment advisory and support services
(f) Promote Nigeria as investment destination internationally
(g) Address investment barriers and regulatory impediments
(h) Coordinate One-Stop Investment Centre (OSIC) for approvals
(i) Issue business permits and expatriate quotas
(j) Support investment protection and guarantees
(k) Promote priority sectors (agriculture, manufacturing, ICT, renewable energy)
(l) Facilitate investment in Free Trade Zones and EPZs
(m) Collaborate with states on investment promotion
(n) Address poor infrastructure deterring investment
(o) Tackle insecurity concerns affecting investor confidence
(p) Combat multiple taxation burdening businesses
(q) Address corruption and regulatory burdens
(r) Improve ease of doing business rankings
(s) Increase FDI inflows (currently ~$2B vs. potential)
(t) Advance investment attraction and climate improvement despite infrastructure, insecurity and regulatory challenges deterring foreign investment

## 725. NIGERIA OFFICE FOR TRADE NEGOTIATIONS (NOTN)

**MDA Code:** 222031001

Nigeria Office for Trade Negotiations is federal agency coordinating international trade negotiations and commercial diplomacy. Located in Abuja, operating under Federal Ministry of Industry, Trade and Investment. Established to negotiate trade agreements, protect Nigerian interests and coordinate WTO matters. Coordinates Nigeria's World Trade Organization (WTO) participation. Negotiates bilateral and multilateral trade agreements. Represents Nigeria in African Continental Free Trade Area (AfCFTA) negotiations. Negotiates Economic Partnership Agreements with EU. Coordinates regional trade (ECOWAS) negotiations. Protects Nigerian industries in trade negotiations. Addresses trade disputes and WTO dispute settlement. Promotes Nigerian export interests internationally. Negotiates market access for Nigerian products. Coordinates tariff negotiations and trade liberalization. Collaborates with MDAs on trade policy positions. Addresses challenges from complex trade rules, limited negotiating capacity and balancing liberalization with protecting local industries. Nigeria's large market makes it attractive to trading partners. AfCFTA creates continent-wide free trade area. Critical for trade diplomacy, protecting economic interests and negotiating favorable trade agreements.

**Core Mandate:** To coordinate trade negotiations, represent Nigeria in WTO and trade forums, negotiate agreements, protect Nigerian interests and advance trade diplomacy.

**Statutory Functions:**
(a) Coordinate international trade negotiations for Nigeria
(b) Represent Nigeria in World Trade Organization (WTO) negotiations
(c) Negotiate bilateral trade agreements with countries
(d) Participate in multilateral trade negotiations
(e) Negotiate African Continental Free Trade Area (AfCFTA) implementation
(f) Coordinate Economic Partnership Agreements with European Union
(g) Represent Nigeria in ECOWAS regional trade negotiations
(h) Protect Nigerian industries in trade liberalization
(i) Address trade disputes and WTO dispute settlement
(j) Promote Nigerian export interests in trade talks
(k) Negotiate market access for Nigerian products abroad
(l) Coordinate tariff negotiations and customs duties
(m) Develop Nigeria's trade negotiating positions
(n) Collaborate with MDAs on trade policy coordination
(o) Analyze trade agreement impacts on Nigerian economy
(p) Promote favorable trade terms for Nigerian businesses
(q) Address trade barriers affecting Nigerian exports
(r) Coordinate non-tariff barriers negotiations
(s) Build trade negotiation capacity
(t) Advance trade diplomacy negotiating favorable agreements protecting Nigerian economic interests in WTO, AfCFTA and bilateral trade forums

## 676. NATIONAL PRODUCTIVITY CENTRE (NPC)

**MDA Code:** 227004001

National Productivity Centre is federal agency promoting productivity improvement, efficiency and competitiveness across sectors. Operating under Federal Ministry of Labour and Employment. Established to enhance productivity in industries, public sector and economy. Conducts productivity training and capacity building. Promotes productivity measurement and benchmarking. Researches productivity trends and best practices. Provides consultancy on productivity improvement to organizations. Promotes quality management and ISO standards adoption. Conducts Kaizen and continuous improvement training. Promotes lean manufacturing and waste reduction. Organizes National Productivity Day celebrating productivity achievements. Awards National Productivity Order of Merit to productive organizations. Conducts productivity conferences and workshops. Promotes workplace productivity and employee engagement. Addresses low productivity - Nigeria's productivity lags regional peers. Promotes technology adoption improving efficiency. Collaborates with industries on productivity enhancement. Addresses challenges from limited awareness, resistance to productivity measurement and weak productivity culture. Productivity improvement critical for competitiveness, economic growth and industrial development. Critical for productivity enhancement, efficiency improvement and promoting competitive economy.

## 618. NATIONAL DIRECTORATE OF EMPLOYMENT (NDE)

**MDA Code:** 227005001

National Directorate of Employment is federal agency addressing unemployment through skills training, job creation and vocational programs. Headquartered in Abuja with offices nationwide, operating under Federal Ministry of Labour and Employment. Established in 1986 to tackle youth unemployment and provide skills for job seekers. Operates four main programs: Vocational Skills Development (VSD), Small Scale Enterprises (SSE), Special Public Works (SPW), and Rural Employment Promotion (REP). Provides free vocational skills training in trades (tailoring, welding, carpentry, etc.). Supports entrepreneurship and business startup training. Provides small business loans and grants to trained beneficiaries. Organizes public works programs creating temporary jobs. Promotes agricultural employment in rural areas. Conducts graduate attachment programs. Addresses youth unemployment estimated 40%+. Collaborates with states, private sector and training institutions. Faces challenges from limited funding, inadequate job creation and sustainability of trained beneficiaries' businesses. Nigeria has massive unemployment crisis with limited formal sector jobs. Critical for skills development, entrepreneurship and reducing unemployment especially among youth.

**Core Mandate:** To address unemployment through skills training, support entrepreneurship, create jobs, provide vocational education and promote self-employment reducing joblessness especially among youth.

**Statutory Functions:**
(a) Address unemployment through skills training and job creation programs
(b) Operate Vocational Skills Development (VSD) program
(c) Provide free vocational training in tailoring, fashion design
(d) Train in welding, metalwork and fabrication skills
(e) Provide carpentry, building and construction training
(f) Operate Small Scale Enterprises (SSE) program
(g) Support entrepreneurship and business startup training
(h) Provide small business loans to trained beneficiaries
(i) Train on business management and financial literacy
(j) Operate Special Public Works (SPW) program
(k) Organize labor-intensive public works creating temporary jobs
(l) Operate Rural Employment Promotion (REP) program
(m) Promote agricultural employment in rural areas
(n) Support youth in agriculture programs
(o) Conduct graduate attachment and internship programs
(p) Address youth unemployment (40%+ unemployment rate)
(q) Collaborate with states on employment generation
(r) Partner with private sector on job placement
(s) Monitor trained beneficiaries' employment outcomes
(t) Advance employment generation and skills development reducing Nigeria's massive unemployment crisis through vocational training, entrepreneurship support and job creation programs

## 723. NIGERIA NATURAL MEDICINE DEVELOPMENT AGENCY (NNMDA)

**MDA Code:** 228004001

Nigeria Natural Medicine Development Agency is federal agency promoting traditional medicine, herbal remedies and natural products development. Operating under Federal Ministry of Science and Technology. Established to regulate traditional medicine, develop herbal drugs and integrate traditional healing with modern healthcare. Regulates traditional medicine practice and practitioners. Promotes research on medicinal plants and natural products. Develops herbal medicines from Nigerian plants. Validates traditional medicine claims scientifically. Registers traditional medicine practitioners. Promotes integration of traditional and orthodox medicine. Develops quality standards for herbal products. Conducts clinical trials on traditional remedies. Documents traditional medical knowledge. Promotes cultivation of medicinal plants. Addresses safety and efficacy of herbal products. Collaborates with traditional healers and herbalists. Works with NAFDAC on herbal product registration. Addresses challenges from lack of regulation, unverified claims, quality control gaps and resistance from orthodox medicine. Many Nigerians use traditional medicine especially rural populations. Critical for traditional medicine development, herbal products safety and integrating traditional healing into healthcare system.

## 686. NATIONAL SPACE RESEARCH AND DEVELOPMENT AGENCY (NASRDA)

**MDA Code:** 228005001

National Space Research and Development Agency is federal agency developing Nigeria's space program and satellite technology. Headquartered in Abuja with centers nationwide, operating under Federal Ministry of Science and Technology. Established to develop indigenous space capabilities, operate satellites and apply space technology to development. Operates Nigerian satellites (NigeriaSat series, NigComSat). Conducts space research and satellite technology development. Provides satellite data for agriculture, environment, disaster management and security. Operates African Regional Centre for Space Science and Technology Education. Develops earth observation applications. Provides remote sensing data for land use planning. Monitors environmental changes using satellite imagery. Supports disaster response with satellite data. Develops satellite ground stations and control centers. Trains space scientists and engineers. Collaborates with international space agencies. Nigeria launched first satellite NigeriaSat-1 (2003). Operates communication satellites for telecommunications. Addresses challenges from satellite failures, limited commercialization and funding constraints. Space technology applications include weather forecasting, border surveillance, agricultural monitoring. Critical for space technology development, satellite operations and applying space science to national development.

**Core Mandate:** To develop space technology, operate satellites, conduct space research, provide satellite data applications and build Nigeria's indigenous space capabilities.

(b) Operate Nigerian satellites (NigeriaSat series)

(d) Conduct space research and satellite development
(e) Provide satellite data for agriculture monitoring
(f) Use satellite imagery for environmental monitoring
(g) Support disaster management with earth observation data
(h) Provide satellite data for security and border surveillance
(i) Operate African Regional Centre for Space Science and Technology
(j) Develop earth observation and remote sensing applications
(k) Provide satellite data for land use planning
(l) Monitor deforestation and environmental changes
(m) Support weather forecasting with satellite data
(n) Develop satellite ground stations and control centers
(o) Train space scientists, engineers and technologists
(p) Conduct research on space science and technology
(q) Collaborate with international space agencies
(r) Promote space technology commercialization
(s) Develop satellite manufacturing capabilities
(t) Advance space technology capacity operating satellites providing earth observation data supporting agriculture, environment, disaster management and security despite funding constraints

## 594. NATIONAL BIOTECHNOLOGY DEVELOPMENT AGENCY (NABDA)

**MDA Code:** 228008001

National Biotechnology Development Agency is federal agency promoting biotechnology research, development and commercialization. Headquartered in Abuja with biotechnology centers nationwide, operating under Federal Ministry of Science and Technology. Established to harness biotechnology for national development addressing food security, health and industrial challenges. Operates biotechnology research centers conducting agricultural, medical and industrial biotechnology. Develops genetically modified crops including disease-resistant varieties. Conducts vaccine development and biopharmaceutical research. Researches industrial biotechnology for bio-based products. Promotes tissue culture for crop propagation. Develops biosensors and diagnostic technologies. Provides biotechnology training and capacity building. Operates modern biotechnology laboratories with genetic engineering facilities. Collaborates with universities, research institutes and biotech companies. Promotes bioentrepreneurship and biotechnology commercialization. Addresses challenges from limited commercialization, biosafety regulations and technology transfer gaps. Complements NBMA's regulatory role focusing on research and development. Critical for biotechnology innovation, technology development and applying genetic engineering to Nigeria's development challenges.

(t) Advance biotechnology innovation addressing food security, health and industrial development through research, training and commercialization

## 604. NATIONAL CENTRE FOR TECHNOLOGY MANAGEMENT (NACETEM), ILE-IFE

**MDA Code:** 228033001

National Centre for Technology Management is federal research institution specializing in technology policy, innovation systems and technology management. Located at Obafemi Awolowo University, Ile-Ife, Osun State, operating under Federal Ministry of Science and Technology. Established to conduct research on technology development, innovation policy and science-technology-society interactions. Conducts policy research on science, technology and innovation (STI). Studies innovation systems and technology transfer mechanisms. Researches technology management for industrial development. Conducts technology foresight and futures studies. Studies intellectual property and patent systems. Researches entrepreneurship and technology commercialization. Provides policy advice on STI to government. Conducts capacity building on technology management. Studies traditional knowledge and indigenous innovations. Researches gender and technology issues. Collaborates with universities, research institutes and international STI organizations. Addresses challenges from limited policy uptake, weak innovation systems and technology transfer gaps. Critical for evidence-based STI policy, innovation promotion and managing technology for development.

## 737. NIGERIAN BUILDING AND ROAD RESEARCH INSTITUTE (NBRRI), LAGOS

**MDA Code:** 228041001

Nigerian Building and Road Research Institute is federal research institution conducting construction materials and infrastructure research. Located in Lagos, operating under Federal Ministry of Science and Technology. Established to research building materials, road construction technologies and infrastructure development. Conducts research on construction materials including cement, concrete and aggregates. Develops low-cost housing materials and technologies. Researches sustainable building materials from local resources. Studies road construction and pavement design. Develops asphalt and bitumen technologies. Researches building failures and structural integrity. Promotes use of local building materials. Develops earth construction and compressed earth blocks. Researches climate-appropriate building designs. Studies building standards and construction codes. Provides materials testing and quality control services. Collaborates with construction companies and ministries. Addresses Nigeria's housing deficit requiring affordable building materials. Poor construction quality causes building collapses. Road infrastructure deteriorates rapidly requiring improved materials. Critical for construction technology, housing development and infrastructure quality through research despite implementation gaps.

## 659. NATIONAL OFFICE FOR TECHNOLOGY ACQUISITION AND PROMOTION (NOTAP)

**MDA Code:** 228043001

National Office for Technology Acquisition and Promotion is federal agency regulating technology transfer and promoting technology development. Located in Abuja, operating under Federal Ministry of Science and Technology. Established to regulate technology transfer agreements, promote local technology development and reduce technology dependence. Registers and approves technology transfer agreements between Nigerian companies and foreign technology providers. Regulates foreign technical assistance and technology licensing. Promotes technology acquisition on favorable terms protecting Nigerian interests. Prevents exploitative technology transfer contracts. Monitors technology payments and royalties to foreign entities. Promotes local technology development and innovation. Encourages technology adaptation and reverse engineering. Supports indigenous technology capacity building. Collaborates with industries on technology development. Addresses technology dependence - Nigeria imports most technologies. Promotes local content in technology contracts. Addresses challenges from weak negotiating capacity, limited local technology base and foreign technology dominance. Technology transfer critical for industrialization but must benefit Nigeria not just enrich foreign firms. Critical for technology development, reducing dependence and ensuring technology transfer supports Nigeria's industrial and technological advancement.

**Core Mandate:** To regulate technology transfer, approve technology agreements, promote local technology development, protect Nigerian interests and reduce technology dependence supporting industrialization.

**Statutory Functions:**
(a) Regulate technology transfer and technology licensing in Nigeria
(b) Register and approve technology transfer agreements
(c) Review contracts between Nigerian firms and foreign technology providers
(d) Regulate foreign technical assistance and consultancy agreements
(e) Promote technology acquisition on favorable commercial terms
(f) Prevent exploitative technology transfer contracts
(g) Monitor technology payments and royalty fees to foreign entities
(h) Promote local technology development and innovation
(i) Encourage technology adaptation and indigenization
(j) Support reverse engineering and technology learning
(k) Build indigenous technology capacity
(l) Collaborate with industries on technology development
(m) Promote local content in technology procurement
(n) Address technology dependence on foreign sources
(o) Negotiate fair technology transfer terms protecting Nigerian interests
(p) Monitor technology performance and implementation
(q) Promote South-South technology cooperation
(r) Support technology start-ups and innovation
(s) Address intellectual property in technology agreements
(t) Advance technology development and industrialization through regulated technology transfer reducing dependence ensuring agreements benefit Nigeria's technological advancement not just foreign technology providers

## 678. NATIONAL RESEARCH INSTITUTE FOR CHEMICAL TECHNOLOGY (NARICT), ZARIA

**MDA Code:** 228044001

National Research Institute for Chemical Technology is federal institution conducting chemical research and developing chemical industries. Located in Zaria, Kaduna State, operating under Federal Ministry of Science and Technology. Established to conduct applied chemical research, develop chemical processes and support chemical industries. Conducts research on industrial chemicals production. Develops pharmaceutical chemicals and drug intermediates. Researches petrochemicals and petroleum refining. Conducts polymer and plastics research. Develops agrochemicals including fertilizers and pesticides. Researches natural products chemistry from Nigerian plants. Conducts materials chemistry and nanomaterials research. Develops chemical processes for industrial applications. Researches water treatment and environmental chemistry. Provides analytical chemistry services and testing. Collaborates with chemical industries on product development. Trains chemists and chemical engineers. Addresses chemical industry underdevelopment - Nigeria imports most chemicals despite raw materials. Promotes local chemical manufacturing reducing import dependence. Faces challenges from limited commercialization, weak industry linkages and funding constraints. Critical for chemical industry development, import substitution and chemical research supporting industrialization.

**Core Mandate:** To conduct chemical research, develop chemical processes, support chemical industries, promote local manufacturing and advance Nigeria's chemical technology capacity.

(b) Develop industrial chemicals production processes

(e) Develop polymer and plastics manufacturing technologies
(f) Research agrochemicals including fertilizers and pesticides
(g) Develop natural products chemistry from Nigerian plants
(h) Conduct materials chemistry and nanomaterials research
(i) Research catalysis and chemical reaction engineering
(j) Develop chemical processes for industrial applications
(k) Research water treatment chemicals and technologies
(l) Conduct environmental chemistry and pollution control research

(n) Collaborate with chemical industries on product development

(p) Promote local chemical manufacturing
(q) Address Nigeria's chemical import dependence
(r) Develop chemical industry value chains
(s) Conduct pilot plant trials for chemical processes
(t) Advance chemical industry development through research supporting local manufacturing, import substitution and industrial chemical production reducing dependence on imported chemicals

## 751. NIGERIAN INSTITUTE FOR TRYPANOSOMIASIS RESEARCH (NITR), KADUNA

**MDA Code:** 228045001

Nigerian Institute for Trypanosomiasis Research is specialized federal research institution studying sleeping sickness and related diseases. Located in Kaduna, operating under Federal Ministry of Science and Technology. Established to research trypanosomiasis (sleeping sickness), develop control methods and support livestock health. Researches human African trypanosomiasis (sleeping sickness). Studies animal trypanosomiasis affecting livestock (Nagana disease). Develops diagnostic tests for trypanosome infections. Researches tsetse fly biology and control methods. Develops vector control strategies. Researches drug resistance in trypanosomes. Studies endemic areas and disease transmission. Develops sustainable control programs. Researches livestock productivity impacts. Collaborates with WHO on sleeping sickness elimination. Addresses challenges from limited funding, tsetse fly persistence and disease endemic areas. Sleeping sickness eliminated in many regions but endemic pockets remain. Animal trypanosomiasis constrains livestock productivity. Critical for disease control, livestock health and supporting elimination of sleeping sickness despite resource constraints and endemic persistence.

**Core Mandate:** To research trypanosomiasis, develop sleeping sickness control methods, study tsetse flies, support livestock health and advance disease elimination efforts.

**Statutory Functions:**
(a) Research human African trypanosomiasis (sleeping sickness)
(b) Study animal trypanosomiasis (Nagana disease) affecting livestock
(c) Develop diagnostic tests for trypanosome infections
(d) Research tsetse fly biology, ecology and behavior
(e) Develop tsetse fly control and eradication methods
(f) Research vector control strategies and technologies
(g) Study insecticide resistance in tsetse flies
(h) Research drug resistance in trypanosomes
(i) Develop new drugs and treatments
(j) Study endemic areas and disease transmission patterns
(k) Develop sustainable trypanosomiasis control programs
(l) Research livestock productivity impacts from trypanosomiasis
(m) Study economic burden of animal trypanosomiasis
(n) Collaborate with WHO on sleeping sickness elimination
(o) Engage FAO on animal disease control
(p) Address limited research funding constraints
(q) Combat tsetse fly persistence in endemic zones
(r) Support livestock development in tsetse-infested areas
(s) Promote integrated pest management approaches
(t) Advance trypanosomiasis control and elimination through research supporting livestock health despite funding constraints and disease persistence

## 720. NIGERIA INSTITUTE OF LEATHER AND SCIENCE TECHNOLOGY (NILEST)

**MDA Code:** 228050001

Nigeria Institute of Leather and Science Technology is federal training institution for leather technology and footwear manufacturing. Operating under Federal Ministry of Science and Technology. Established to develop leather industry skills, train leather technologists and support leather sector. Trains leather technologists and tanners. Provides footwear design and manufacturing training. Teaches leather processing and tanning technologies. Offers training in leather goods manufacturing. Provides training on hides and skins processing. Trains on leather chemistry and materials. Offers shoemaking and footwear production programs. Provides leather quality control training. Supports leather industry workforce development. Collaborates with leather industries and footwear companies. Nigeria has large livestock population (cattle, goats, sheep) producing hides and skins. Most exported as raw materials despite potential for leather products manufacturing. Sokoto, Kano famous for leather goods (traditional Moroccan leather). Faces challenges from leather industry underdevelopment, competition from imported shoes/leather products and limited industrialization. Critical for leather sector development, value addition, footwear manufacturing and reducing raw hides/skins exports through skilled leather workforce.

**Core Mandate:** To train leather technologists, develop footwear manufacturing skills, support leather industry, promote value addition and advance Nigeria's leather sector development.

(b) Provide footwear design and manufacturing training
(c) Teach leather processing and tanning technologies

(i) Train on leather finishing and dyeing techniques
(j) Support leather industry workforce development

(l) Partner with footwear manufacturing companies
(m) Promote value addition to hides and skins
(n) Address export of raw hides instead of finished leather
(o) Support Sokoto and Kano traditional leather industries
(p) Develop modern leather technology skills
(q) Promote leather sector industrialization
(r) Address competition from imported footwear
(s) Support local shoe manufacturing
(t) Advance leather sector development training workforce for footwear manufacturing and leather processing reducing raw materials export promoting value addition

Nigeria Institute of Science Laboratory Technology is federal training institution for laboratory scientists and technicians. ## 741. NIGERIAN COUNCIL OF FOOD SCIENCE AND TECHNOLOGY

**MDA Code:** 228079001

Nigerian Council of Food Science and Technology is professional body regulating food science profession and promoting food technology. Operating under Federal Ministry of Science and Technology. Established to regulate food scientists, promote food science education and advance food technology development. Regulates food science and technology profession. Registers qualified food scientists and technologists. Accredits food science academic programs in universities. Promotes food safety and food quality standards. Advances food processing and preservation technologies. Supports food industry research and development. Promotes value addition in food processing. Collaborates with NAFDAC on food safety. Conducts continuing professional development for food scientists. Addresses food safety challenges and foodborne illnesses. Promotes modern food technology reducing post-harvest losses. Supports agro-processing and food manufacturing. Addresses challenges from limited food technology adoption, weak food processing sector and food safety gaps. Nigeria's food industry underdeveloped despite agricultural potential. Critical for food safety, food technology advancement and supporting food processing industry through professional regulation and technology promotion.

## 757. NIGERIAN INSTITUTE OF TRANSPORT TECHNOLOGY (NITT), ZARIA

**MDA Code:** 229002001

Nigerian Institute of Transport Technology is federal research and training institution for transportation sector. Located in Zaria, Kaduna State, operating under Federal Ministry of Transportation. Established to conduct transport research, develop transport technologies and train transport professionals. Conducts research on transport systems and infrastructure. Develops vehicle technologies and automotive engineering. Researches railway systems and rail transport. Studies maritime transport and shipping. Researches aviation technology and air transport. Provides transport engineering training programs. Develops transport planning and policy research. Trains transport managers and operators. Researches traffic management and road safety. Provides continuing education for transport professionals. Collaborates with transport companies and agencies. Addresses challenges from limited funding, outdated equipment and weak transport sector. Nigeria's transport infrastructure poor - bad roads, limited railways, inadequate ports. Critical for transport technology, professional training and supporting transport sector development despite infrastructure challenges.

**Core Mandate:** To conduct transport research, develop transport technologies, train transport professionals, support transport planning and advance Nigeria's transport sector capacity.

## 762. NIGERIAN RAILWAY CORPORATION (NRC)

**MDA Code:** 229003001

Nigerian Railway Corporation is federal agency operating railway services and managing rail infrastructure. Operating under Federal Ministry of Transportation. Established to provide rail transport, maintain railway infrastructure and support railway development. Operates passenger rail services on existing routes. Provides freight rail transport for cargo. Maintains narrow gauge railway network (3,505 km inherited colonial lines). Operates Lagos-Ibadan standard gauge railway (recently commissioned). Manages Abuja-Kaduna standard gauge line. Operates Itakpe-Ajaokuta-Warri railway. Provides commuter rail services in Lagos. Maintains railway stations and terminals. Addresses railway infrastructure decay from decades of neglect. Promotes railway as road transport alternative. Supports economic development through rail connectivity. Faces massive challenges - colonial narrow gauge network collapsed, decades of underinvestment, competing with road transport, limited modern infrastructure. Railway renaissance underway with Chinese-funded standard gauge projects but progress slow. Railway once vital but declined to near-total collapse before recent revival efforts. Critical for transport diversification, economic development and reviving railway infrastructure despite decades of neglect.

**Core Mandate:** To operate railway services, provide passenger and freight rail transport, maintain railway infrastructure and support railway sector revival and development.

**Statutory Functions:**
(a) Operate passenger railway services on existing routes
(b) Provide freight rail transport for cargo and goods
(c) Maintain narrow gauge railway network (3,505 km colonial lines)
(d) Operate Lagos-Ibadan standard gauge railway line
(e) Manage Abuja-Kaduna standard gauge railway
(f) Operate Itakpe-Ajaokuta-Warri railway corridor
(g) Provide Lagos commuter rail and mass transit services
(h) Maintain railway stations, terminals and facilities
(i) Ensure railway safety and accident prevention
(j) Address railway infrastructure decay from decades of neglect
(k) Promote railway as alternative to road transport
(l) Support economic development through rail connectivity
(m) Facilitate cargo movement reducing road congestion
(n) Operate railway workshops and maintenance depots
(o) Train railway personnel and operators
(p) Address collapsed narrow gauge network requiring rehabilitation
(q) Implement railway modernization and expansion projects
(r) Collaborate with Chinese contractors on standard gauge construction
(s) Address decades of underinvestment in railways
(t) Advance railway revival and transport diversification despite decades of collapse implementing standard gauge modernization addressing infrastructure decay

## 760. NIGERIAN METEOROLOGICAL AGENCY (NiMet)

**MDA Code:** 230002001

Nigerian Meteorological Agency is federal agency providing weather forecasting, climate services and meteorological information. Operating under Federal Ministry of Aviation. Established to provide weather services, support aviation safety, agriculture and disaster preparedness. Provides weather forecasts and meteorological information. Issues daily weather predictions nationwide. Supports aviation with aeronautical meteorology. Provides climate data and climate change information. Issues seasonal rainfall predictions for agriculture. Provides flood forecasts and extreme weather warnings. Operates weather stations and observation networks. Monitors atmospheric conditions and climate patterns. Collaborates with NIMET and international meteorological organizations. Supports disaster preparedness with early warnings. Provides marine weather forecasts for shipping. Addresses challenges from limited weather stations, equipment needs, forecast accuracy and climate data gaps. Accurate weather forecasting critical for aviation, agriculture (planting/harvesting), flooding preparedness. Climate change increasing extreme weather requiring better forecasting. Critical for weather services, climate information and disaster early warning despite infrastructure and accuracy challenges.

**Core Mandate:** To provide weather forecasting and climate services, support aviation meteorology, issue extreme weather warnings, provide agricultural climate information and ensure meteorological capacity.

**Statutory Functions:**
(a) Provide weather forecasts and meteorological information
(b) Issue daily weather predictions for Nigerian cities
(c) Provide aeronautical meteorology for aviation safety
(d) Support flight operations with weather briefings
(e) Provide climate data and climate change information
(f) Issue seasonal rainfall predictions for agriculture
(g) Provide planting season forecasts for farmers
(h) Issue flood forecasts and flood warnings
(i) Provide extreme weather alerts (storms, heavy rains)
(j) Operate weather observation stations nationwide
(k) Maintain automated weather stations and radars
(l) Monitor atmospheric conditions and climate patterns
(m) Collaborate with WMO (World Meteorological Organization)
(n) Engage regional meteorological centers
(o) Support disaster preparedness with early warnings
(p) Provide marine weather forecasts for shipping and offshore
(q) Address limited weather observation stations
(r) Upgrade meteorological equipment and infrastructure
(s) Improve forecast accuracy and reliability
(t) Advance meteorological services providing accurate weather forecasts supporting aviation, agriculture and disaster preparedness despite station limitations and accuracy challenges

## 739. NIGERIAN COLLEGE OF AVIATION TECHNOLOGY (NCAT), ZARIA

**MDA Code:** 230003001

Nigerian College of Aviation Technology is premier federal institution training aviation professionals and aircraft engineers. Located in Zaria, Kaduna State, operating under Federal Ministry of Aviation. Established 1964 as West Africa's foremost aviation training center. Trains aircraft maintenance engineers and technicians. Provides pilot training and licensing programs. Trains air traffic controllers for aviation safety. Offers aircraft engineering programs (mechanical, avionics, electrical). Provides continuing education for aviation professionals. Issues licenses and ratings for pilots and engineers. Trains cabin crew and flight attendants. Collaborates with NCAA on aviation training standards. Operates training aircraft and simulators. Provides ICAO-compliant aviation training. Trains for Nigerian airlines and regional carriers. Addresses aviation workforce shortages. Faces challenges from aging training aircraft, equipment maintenance and competition from foreign training institutions. Many Nigerian pilots train abroad. NCAT legacy institution but requires modernization. Critical for aviation safety, professional workforce and providing West African aviation training despite infrastructure needs.

**Core Mandate:** To train aviation professionals, provide aircraft engineering education, train pilots and air traffic controllers, ensure aviation safety and serve West African aviation training needs.

**Statutory Functions:**

(b) Provide pilot training and licensing programs
(c) Train air traffic controllers for aviation safety

(e) Provide continuing education for aviation professionals
(f) Issue licenses and ratings for pilots and engineers
(g) Train cabin crew and flight attendants
(h) Provide ground handling and airport operations training
(i) Operate as ICAO-compliant aviation training center
(j) Collaborate with NCAA on aviation training standards
(k) Operate training aircraft for flight instruction
(l) Provide flight simulators for pilot training
(m) Train engineers for Nigerian airlines
(n) Serve West African regional aviation training needs
(o) Address aviation workforce shortages in Nigeria
(p) Provide refresher training for licensed personnel
(q) Conduct aviation safety training programs
(r) Address aging training aircraft requiring replacement
(s) Compete with foreign aviation training institutions
(t) Advance aviation safety and professional workforce through comprehensive training despite infrastructure modernization needs maintaining West African aviation training legacy

## 706. NIGERIA AIRSPACE MANAGEMENT AGENCY (NAMA)

**MDA Code:** 230004001

Nigeria Airspace Management Agency is federal agency managing Nigerian airspace and providing air traffic control services. Operating under Federal Ministry of Aviation. Established to manage airspace, provide air navigation services and ensure aviation safety. Provides air traffic control services at Nigerian airports. Manages Nigerian airspace and flight information regions. Ensures aircraft separation and collision avoidance. Operates control towers at major airports (Lagos, Abuja, Kano, Port Harcourt). Provides approach and departure control services. Operates area control centers managing en-route traffic. Maintains air navigation equipment and radar systems. Provides aeronautical information and weather services. Coordinates with regional air navigation service providers. Ensures compliance with ICAO international standards. Addresses air traffic delays and congestion especially Lagos airspace. Collaborates with NCAA on aviation safety. Faces challenges from aging equipment, radar coverage gaps, power supply disruptions and capacity constraints. Lagos airspace among world's busiest requiring modern systems. Critical for aviation safety, airspace management and preventing mid-air collisions through professional air traffic control.

**Core Mandate:** To manage Nigerian airspace, provide air traffic control, ensure aircraft separation, operate navigation systems and guarantee aviation safety through airspace management.

**Statutory Functions:**
(a) Manage Nigerian airspace and flight information regions
(b) Provide air traffic control services at Nigerian airports
(c) Ensure aircraft separation and collision avoidance
(d) Operate control towers at major airports (Lagos, Abuja, Kano, Port Harcourt)
(e) Provide approach and departure control services
(f) Operate area control centers managing en-route air traffic
(g) Maintain air navigation equipment and systems
(h) Operate radar systems for aircraft tracking
(i) Provide aeronautical information services
(j) Disseminate meteorological information to pilots
(k) Coordinate with regional air navigation providers
(l) Ensure compliance with ICAO international standards
(m) Address air traffic delays and airspace congestion
(n) Manage Lagos airspace (among world's busiest)
(o) Collaborate with NCAA on aviation safety
(p) Operate communications systems for air traffic
(q) Provide flight planning and briefing services
(r) Manage emergency and distress situations
(s) Address aging equipment and radar coverage gaps
(t) Advance aviation safety through professional airspace management preventing collisions ensuring efficient air traffic flow despite equipment and capacity challenges

## 763. NIGERIAN SAFETY INVESTIGATION BUREAU (NSIB)

**MDA Code:** 230006001

Nigerian Safety Investigation Bureau is independent federal agency investigating aviation, marine and railway accidents. Operating autonomously under NSIB Act. Established to investigate transportation accidents, determine causes and improve safety through recommendations. Investigates aircraft accidents and serious incidents. Conducts marine accident investigations. Investigates railway accidents and incidents. Determines accident causes and contributing factors. Issues safety recommendations preventing recurrence. Operates independently from regulatory agencies (NCAA, NIMASA). Publishes accident investigation reports. Promotes transportation safety culture. Collaborates with international investigation agencies (NTSB, AAIB). Implements ICAO Annex 13 standards for aircraft investigations. Addresses challenges from limited investigation capacity, equipment needs and implementation of safety recommendations. Aviation accidents require thorough investigation. Dana Air crash (2012), Associated Airlines crash (2013) highlighted importance. Independence from regulators critical for objective investigations. Critical for transportation safety, accident prevention and ensuring thorough independent investigations despite capacity constraints.

**Core Mandate:** To investigate transportation accidents independently, determine causes, issue safety recommendations, prevent recurrence and advance aviation, marine and railway safety.

**Statutory Functions:**
(a) Investigate aircraft accidents and serious aviation incidents
(b) Conduct marine and maritime accident investigations
(c) Investigate railway accidents and derailments
(d) Determine accident causes and contributing factors
(e) Analyze human factors, mechanical failures and systemic issues
(f) Issue safety recommendations to prevent accident recurrence
(g) Operate independently from regulatory agencies (NCAA, NIMASA, NRC)
(h) Publish comprehensive accident investigation reports
(i) Promote transportation safety culture and awareness
(j) Collaborate with NTSB (US), AAIB (UK) and international investigators
(k) Implement ICAO Annex 13 standards for investigations
(l) Recover flight data recorders (black boxes) from crash sites
(m) Conduct wreckage examination and analysis
(n) Interview witnesses and aviation personnel
(o) Make recommendations to aviation authorities
(p) Address limited investigation capacity and resources
(q) Acquire accident investigation equipment and tools
(r) Promote implementation of safety recommendations
(s) Monitor safety improvements from investigations
(t) Advance transportation safety through independent thorough investigations preventing future accidents despite capacity constraints

## 680. NATIONAL RURAL ELECTRIFICATION AGENCY (NREA)

**MDA Code:** 231003001

National Rural Electrification Agency is federal agency expanding electricity access to rural and underserved areas. Operating under Federal Ministry of Power. Established to electrify rural communities, promote renewable energy and achieve universal electricity access. Implements Rural Electrification Strategy and Implementation Plan. Develops mini-grids for rural communities using solar and hybrid systems. Promotes solar home systems for off-grid households. Constructs grid extension to unelectrified communities. Develops renewable energy solutions (solar, biomass, small hydro). Operates Nigeria Electrification Project with World Bank support. Provides electricity to rural health centers and schools. Supports productive use of electricity in agriculture and SMEs. Collaborates with private sector on mini-grid development. Addresses energy poverty - ~85 million Nigerians lack electricity access, mostly rural. Nigeria has ~43% electricity access rate, among world's lowest. Rural electrification critical for development, education, healthcare and poverty reduction. Faces challenges from funding gaps, difficult terrain, low revenue from poor rural communities and grid infrastructure limitations. Critical for universal electricity access, rural development and energy poverty reduction.

**Core Mandate:** To expand electricity access to rural areas, develop off-grid renewable energy solutions, implement rural electrification projects and achieve universal electricity access.

**Statutory Functions:**
(a) Expand electricity access to rural and underserved communities
(b) Implement Rural Electrification Strategy and Implementation Plan
(c) Develop solar mini-grids for off-grid rural communities
(d) Promote solar home systems for households without electricity
(e) Construct grid extension to unelectrified communities
(f) Develop renewable energy solutions (solar, biomass, small hydro)
(g) Operate Nigeria Electrification Project with World Bank funding
(h) Provide electricity to rural health centers and clinics
(i) Electrify rural schools improving education quality
(j) Support productive use of electricity in agriculture
(k) Electrify SMEs and rural businesses
(l) Collaborate with private sector on mini-grid development
(m) Promote public-private partnerships in rural electrification
(n) Address energy poverty affecting 85+ million Nigerians
(o) Increase Nigeria's 43% electricity access rate
(p) Develop sustainable business models for rural electricity
(q) Promote energy efficiency in rural electrification
(r) Conduct awareness on renewable energy benefits
(s) Monitor and evaluate rural electrification projects
(t) Advance universal electricity access and rural development electrifying communities addressing energy poverty (85M+ without electricity) through renewable mini-grids and grid extension despite funding and sustainability challenges

## 743. NIGERIAN ELECTRICITY MANAGEMENT SERVICES AGENCY (NEMSA)

**MDA Code:** 231005001

Nigerian Electricity Management Services Agency is federal regulatory agency ensuring electrical installations safety and standards compliance. Operating under Federal Ministry of Power. Established to regulate electrical installations, enforce safety standards and prevent electrical hazards. Regulates electrical installations in buildings and facilities. Enforces electrical wiring and safety standards. Inspects electrical installations ensuring compliance. Licenses electricians and electrical contractors. Prevents electrical fires and electrocution accidents. Regulates meter installation and connections. Addresses unsafe electrical practices and illegal connections. Promotes electrical safety awareness and education. Conducts electrical accident investigations. Collaborates with DisCos on safe connections. Addresses challenges from widespread illegal connections, unqualified electricians, poor enforcement and electrical safety culture gaps. Electrical accidents cause deaths and property damage. Many buildings have substandard wiring. Illegal connections prevalent. Critical for electrical safety, preventing accidents and ensuring safe electrical installations despite enforcement challenges and poor safety compliance.

**Core Mandate:** To regulate electrical installations safety, enforce electrical standards, license electricians, prevent electrical accidents and ensure safe electricity use nationwide.

**Statutory Functions:**

(b) Enforce electrical wiring and installation safety standards
(c) Inspect electrical installations ensuring code compliance
(d) License electricians and electrical contractors
(e) Prevent electrical fires from faulty installations
(f) Address electrocution accidents and electrical hazards
(g) Regulate electricity meter installation and connections
(h) Address unsafe electrical practices in buildings
(i) Combat illegal electrical connections and bypasses
(j) Promote electrical safety awareness and public education
(k) Conduct electrical accident investigations
(l) Enforce penalties for unsafe electrical work
(m) Collaborate with Distribution Companies on safe connections
(n) Regulate residential, commercial and industrial installations
(o) Promote use of qualified electricians
(p) Address widespread illegal electricity connections
(q) Combat unqualified electricians doing unsafe work
(r) Strengthen electrical safety culture and compliance
(s) Inspect new buildings for electrical safety certification
(t) Advance electrical safety preventing accidents and fires through installation regulation despite enforcement challenges and poor compliance culture

## 674. NATIONAL POWER TRAINING INSTITUTE OF NIGERIA (NAPTIN)

**MDA Code:** 231010001

National Power Training Institute of Nigeria is federal training institution developing power sector workforce. Operating under Federal Ministry of Power. Established to train engineers, technicians and professionals for electricity generation, transmission and distribution. Provides training for power plant operators and maintenance engineers. Trains electrical engineers on power systems and grid management. Offers programs in power generation technologies. Trains on transmission infrastructure and high-voltage systems. Provides distribution network management training. Conducts renewable energy training (solar, wind, hydro). Trains on smart grid and modern power technologies. Offers metering and billing systems training. Provides power sector safety and occupational health training. Conducts continuing education for power sector workers. Collaborates with electricity generation companies (GenCos), transmission company (TCN) and distribution companies (DisCos). Addresses Nigeria's power crisis - chronic electricity shortages, grid collapses, limited generation capacity (~4,000-5,000 MW for 200M+ population). Power sector requires skilled workforce for improvement. Faces challenges from outdated training equipment and rapidly evolving power technologies. Critical for power sector capacity building, skilled workforce development and supporting electricity sector improvement.

**Core Mandate:** To train power sector professionals, develop electricity workforce, provide technical capacity building and support Nigeria's power sector development through skilled human capital.

**Statutory Functions:**
(a) Train engineers and technicians for power sector
(b) Provide training for power plant operators
(c) Train maintenance engineers for generation facilities

(k) Train on power sector safety and occupational health
(l) Conduct continuing professional development for power workers
(m) Collaborate with Generation Companies (GenCos) on training

(o) Partner with Distribution Companies (DisCos) on capacity building
(p) Address Nigeria's power crisis through workforce development
(q) Train on grid stability and frequency management

(t) Advance power sector capacity addressing electricity crisis (4,000-5,000 MW for 200M+ population) through skilled workforce development supporting generation, transmission and distribution improvement

## 712. NIGERIA ELECTRICITY LIABILITY MANAGEMENT LIMITED (NELMCO)

**MDA Code:** 231011001

Nigeria Electricity Liability Management Limited is federal agency managing legacy debts and liabilities from power sector privatization. Operating under Federal Ministry of Power. Established to manage stranded costs and financial liabilities from unbundling of former Power Holding Company of Nigeria (PHCN). Manages legacy debts from defunct PHCN. Addresses stranded assets and liabilities post-privatization. Manages pension liabilities for former PHCN workers. Settles outstanding obligations to contractors and suppliers. Addresses electricity sector financial legacy issues. Manages gas supply debts and obligations. Coordinates resolution of pre-privatization liabilities. Prevents legacy debts constraining privatized companies. Collaborates with Generation Companies and Distribution Companies. Addresses financial barriers to power sector reform. Manages severance payments for disengaged workers. Power sector privatization 2013 left enormous legacy debts. NELMCO prevents these from undermining sector performance. Faces challenges from enormous debt quantum, limited resources and complex liability structures. Critical for power sector financial sustainability, enabling privatized entities focus on operations and preventing legacy liabilities sabotaging electricity reform.

**Core Mandate:** To manage power sector legacy debts and liabilities, address PHCN obligations, settle pre-privatization claims and enable electricity sector financial sustainability.

**Statutory Functions:**
(a) Manage legacy debts and liabilities from defunct PHCN
(b) Address stranded assets from power sector unbundling
(c) Manage pension liabilities for former PHCN employees
(d) Settle outstanding obligations to contractors and suppliers
(e) Address electricity sector financial legacy issues
(f) Manage gas supply debts and payment obligations
(g) Coordinate resolution of pre-privatization liabilities
(h) Prevent legacy debts constraining privatized GenCos and DisCos
(i) Manage severance payments for disengaged workers
(j) Address redundancy compensation for former PHCN staff
(k) Settle claims from power sector restructuring
(l) Manage stranded generation and transmission assets
(m) Address inherited contractual obligations
(n) Coordinate with Ministry of Finance on debt resolution
(o) Collaborate with Generation Companies on legacy issues
(p) Work with Distribution Companies addressing inherited liabilities
(q) Enable privatized entities focus on operations not legacy debts
(r) Address financial barriers to power sector reform
(s) Manage enormous debt quantum from decades of sector neglect
(t) Advance power sector financial sustainability managing PHCN legacy preventing inherited debts undermining electricity reform and privatization

## 856. TRANSMISSION COMPANY OF NIGERIA (TCN)

**MDA Code:** 231020001

Transmission Company of Nigeria operates national electricity transmission grid. Operates under Federal Ministry of Power. Transmits electricity from generating companies to distribution companies. Operates high voltage transmission lines and substations. Manages national grid system. Successor to PHCN transmission division.

**Core Mandate:** To transmit electricity nationwide, operate transmission grid, wheel power from GenCos to DisCos and ensure grid stability.

(b) Deliver electricity to distribution companies

(g) Ensure grid stability and reliability
(h) Balance electricity supply and demand
(i) Coordinate with generating companies (GenCos)
(j) Coordinate with distribution companies (DisCos)

(l) Build new transmission lines
(m) Build new substations
(n) Upgrade aging transmission infrastructure
(o) Reduce transmission losses
(p) Manage grid collapse incidents
(q) Restore power after grid failures
(r) Operate grid control centers
(s) Monitor real-time electricity flow
(t) Advance electricity transmission through grid operation ensuring power delivery from generation to distribution

## 796. PETROLEUM TRAINING INSTITUTE (PTI), EFFURUN

**MDA Code:** 232003001

Petroleum Training Institute is specialized federal training institution for oil and gas professionals. Located in Effurun, Delta State (Niger Delta oil region), operating under Federal Ministry of Petroleum Resources. Established to train petroleum engineers, technicians and oil industry professionals. Provides diploma and certificate programs in petroleum engineering. Trains oil and gas production technicians. Provides welding, fabrication and NDT training. Trains on Health, Safety and Environment (HSE) management. Supports local content development in oil sector. Collaborates with oil companies on practical training. Provides continuing education for oil workers. Addresses challenges from equipment needs, industry linkages and local content implementation. Nigeria's oil industry requires skilled workforce. Located in Delta State oil heartland. Critical for petroleum sector workforce, local content support and oil industry training despite equipment and industry engagement needs.

## 724. NIGERIA NUCLEAR REGULATORY AUTHORITY (NNRA)

**MDA Code:** 232009001

Nigeria Nuclear Regulatory Authority is independent federal agency regulating nuclear safety and radiation protection. Operating autonomously under Nuclear Safety and Radiation Protection Act. Established to regulate nuclear activities, ensure radiation safety and protect public from radiation hazards. Regulates all nuclear and radiation activities in Nigeria. Licenses radiation facilities including hospitals and industries. Ensures radiation safety in medical, industrial and research applications. Regulates radioactive materials importation and use. Conducts inspections of radiation facilities. Enforces radiation protection standards. Licenses radiation workers and medical physicists. Regulates nuclear medicine departments in hospitals. Ensures safety of X-ray machines and CT scanners. Addresses radiation emergencies and accidents. Monitors environmental radioactivity levels. Collaborates with IAEA on nuclear safety standards. Independent from promotional nuclear agencies (NAEC). Addresses challenges from limited public awareness, inadequate radiation safety culture and enforcement capacity. Radiation widely used in Nigerian hospitals but safety gaps exist. Critical for radiation safety, nuclear regulation and protecting public from radiation hazards.

**Core Mandate:** To regulate nuclear safety and radiation protection, license radiation facilities, ensure radiation safety, protect public from radiation hazards and enforce safety standards.

**Statutory Functions:**

(b) License all radiation facilities including hospitals and industries
(c) Ensure radiation safety in medical applications (radiotherapy, nuclear medicine)

(f) Regulate radioactive materials importation, transport and use

(i) License radiation workers, medical physicists and radiographers
(j) Regulate nuclear medicine departments in hospitals
(k) Ensure safety of X-ray machines, CT scanners and medical imaging
(l) Address radiation emergencies, accidents and incidents
(m) Monitor environmental radioactivity levels
(n) Regulate radioactive waste management and disposal
(o) Enforce physical security of radioactive sources
(p) Prevent illicit trafficking of nuclear and radioactive materials
(q) Collaborate with IAEA on international nuclear safety standards
(r) Maintain independence from nuclear promotional agencies
(s) Promote radiation safety culture in healthcare and industry
(t) Advance radiation safety and nuclear regulation protecting public from radiation hazards despite limited safety awareness

## 689. NATIONAL STEEL RAW MATERIALS EXPLORATION AGENCY (NSRMEA), KADUNA

**MDA Code:** 233004001

National Steel Raw Materials Exploration Agency is federal agency exploring and developing raw materials for steel industry. Located in Kaduna, operating under Federal Ministry of Mines and Steel Development. Established to explore iron ore, limestone, coal and other steel-making raw materials. Conducts geological surveys for iron ore deposits. Explores coal deposits for coking coal (steel production). Identifies limestone deposits for steel flux. Explores dolomite, manganese and other steel raw materials. Conducts feasibility studies on mineral deposits. Develops raw materials supply chains for steel industry. Supports Ajaokuta Steel and Delta Steel raw materials needs. Collaborates with Itakpe Iron Ore Mining on iron ore. Explores coal deposits in Enugu, Kogi and Benue. Identifies refractory materials for steel furnaces. Provides geological data supporting steel industry. Addresses challenges from Ajaokuta Steel non-operation eliminating main customer, limited steel industry development and exploration funding gaps. Nigeria has abundant steel raw materials - iron ore (Itakpe, Agbaja), coal (Enugu, Okaba), limestone - but steel industry dormant. Critical for steel industry feedstock, mineral exploration and supporting steel sector revival when/if operational.

**Core Mandate:** To explore steel raw materials, conduct geological surveys, identify iron ore and coal deposits, develop raw materials supply and support steel industry feedstock needs.

**Statutory Functions:**

(b) Conduct geological surveys for iron ore deposits nationwide
(c) Explore coal deposits for coking coal (steel production)
(d) Identify limestone deposits for steel flux and cement
(e) Explore dolomite, manganese and ferroalloy materials
(f) Conduct feasibility studies on mineral deposits
(g) Develop raw materials supply chains for steel industry
(h) Support Ajaokuta Steel Company raw materials requirements
(i) Collaborate with Delta Steel on raw materials supply
(j) Coordinate with Itakpe Iron Ore Mining Project
(k) Explore coal deposits in Enugu, Kogi and Benue States
(l) Identify refractory materials for steel furnaces
(m) Provide geological data supporting steel investments
(n) Conduct mineral resource assessments
(o) Develop mining feasibility for steel feedstock
(p) Promote sustainable mining of steel raw materials

(r) Support potential steel industry revival with raw materials data
(s) Collaborate with mining companies on steel minerals
(t) Advance steel raw materials development through exploration providing geological data despite steel industry dormancy and Ajaokuta non-operation limiting feedstock demand

## 655. NATIONAL METALLURGICAL DEVELOPMENT CENTRE (NMDC), JOS

**MDA Code:** 233005001

National Metallurgical Development Centre is federal research institution conducting metallurgical research and supporting steel and metal industries. Located in Jos, Plateau State, operating under Federal Ministry of Mines and Steel Development. Established to conduct metallurgy research, develop metal processing technologies and support Nigeria's metallurgical sector. Conducts research on iron and steel production. Researches metal extraction and refining processes. Develops foundry technologies and metal casting. Researches welding and fabrication technologies. Conducts metallurgical testing and quality control. Researches alloy development and materials engineering. Develops corrosion prevention and metal treatment technologies. Supports Ajaokuta Steel and metallurgical industries with technical assistance. Conducts mineral beneficiation research. Provides metallurgical consultancy services. Trains metallurgists and metal workers. Operates pilot plants for metallurgical processes. Collaborates with steel companies and metal fabricators. Addresses challenges from steel sector underdevelopment, Ajaokuta Steel non-operation and limited local metallurgical capacity. Nigeria imports steel and metal products despite iron ore and mineral resources. Critical for steel sector development, metallurgical capacity building and supporting industrialization through metal processing research.

**Core Mandate:** To conduct metallurgical research, develop metal processing technologies, support steel and metal industries, train metallurgists and advance Nigeria's metallurgical sector development.

## 648. NATIONAL IRON ORE MINING PROJECT, ITAKPE

**MDA Code:** 233008001

National Iron Ore Mining Project Itakpe is federal iron ore mining operation providing raw materials for steel production. Located in Itakpe, Kogi State, operating under Federal Ministry of Mines and Steel Development. Established as integrated mining project feeding Ajaokuta Steel Company with iron ore. Itakpe hosts significant iron ore deposits (estimated 200+ million tons). Conducts iron ore mining extraction operations. Processes iron ore concentrating and beneficiating ore. Produces iron ore pellets for steel making. Supplies iron ore to Ajaokuta Steel Company (non-functional). Operates mining infrastructure including crushers and concentrators. Maintains ore handling facilities. Conducts geological surveys and ore reserve assessment. Collaborates with Ajaokuta Steel Project on iron ore supply. Faces challenges from Ajaokuta Steel's non-operational status eliminating main customer, limited alternative markets and infrastructure constraints. Itakpe-Ajaokuta railway constructed for ore transportation but underutilized. Project represents Nigeria's steel industry ambitions - iron ore, steel plant, but both underperforming. Critical for steel sector development, mineral exploitation and providing raw materials for industrialization if steel sector revived.

**Core Mandate:** To mine iron ore at Itakpe, process ore, produce iron ore pellets, supply steel industry and support Nigeria's steel and metallurgical sector development.

**Statutory Functions:**
(a) Conduct iron ore mining extraction at Itakpe deposits
(b) Exploit 200+ million tons iron ore reserves
(c) Process iron ore through crushing and grinding
(d) Beneficiate iron ore concentrating ore content
(e) Produce iron ore pellets for steel making
(f) Supply iron ore to Ajaokuta Steel Company
(g) Operate mining infrastructure including crushers
(h) Maintain ore concentrators and beneficiation plants
(i) Operate ore handling and transportation facilities
(j) Conduct geological surveys and ore reserve assessments
(k) Explore additional iron ore deposits in region
(l) Maintain Itakpe-Ajaokuta railway for ore transport
(m) Ensure sustainable mining practices
(n) Address environmental impacts of mining operations
(o) Develop alternative markets for iron ore
(p) Explore iron ore export opportunities
(q) Collaborate with steel companies on ore supply
(r) Support mining sector employment in Kogi State
(s) Promote mining technology and mechanization
(t) Advance steel sector development providing iron ore raw materials supporting industrialization despite Ajaokuta Steel non-operation challenging project viability

## 688. NATIONAL STEEL COUNCIL (NSC)

**MDA Code:** 233013001

National Steel Council is federal coordinating body for steel industry development and policy. Operating under Federal Ministry of Mines and Steel Development. Established to coordinate steel sector development, revive Ajaokuta Steel and promote iron/steel production. Coordinates steel industry policies and programs. Oversees Ajaokuta Steel Company Limited (non-operational for decades). Coordinates Delta Steel Company operations (also struggling). Promotes local steel production and manufacturing. Develops steel industry master plans. Coordinates iron ore mining for steel production. Promotes backward integration - iron ore to steel products. Regulates steel imports and promotes local content. Supports steel fabrication and downstream industries. Collaborates with Itakpe Iron Ore Mining Project. Addresses Nigeria's steel sector crisis - Ajaokuta Steel never operational despite $8+ billion invested since 1970s-1980s, Delta Steel Company struggling, Nigeria imports steel worth billions annually despite iron ore resources. Russian and Indian companies involved in Ajaokuta saga. Recent attempts at concession and revival. Steel sector represents Nigeria's industrial ambitions vs. reality gap. Critical for industrialization, steel self-sufficiency and reviving moribund steel infrastructure.

**Core Mandate:** To coordinate steel industry development, revive Ajaokuta Steel, promote local steel production, regulate steel sector and advance Nigeria's steel industry revival.

**Statutory Functions:**

(b) Oversee Ajaokuta Steel Company Limited revival efforts
(c) Coordinate Delta Steel Company Aladja operations
(d) Promote local steel production and manufacturing
(e) Develop steel industry master plans and strategies
(f) Coordinate iron ore mining for steel feedstock
(g) Promote backward integration from ore to finished steel
(h) Regulate steel imports and promote import substitution
(i) Support steel fabrication and downstream industries
(j) Collaborate with Itakpe Iron Ore Mining Project
(k) Develop steel value chains and industrial clusters
(l) Address Ajaokuta Steel non-operation despite $8B+ investment
(m) Navigate Ajaokuta concession and partnership agreements
(n) Address Nigeria's steel import dependence (billions annually)
(o) Promote local content in steel procurement
(p) Support steel-consuming industries (construction, manufacturing, automotive)
(q) Develop metallurgical skills and steel workforce
(r) Collaborate with international steel companies
(s) Monitor global steel markets and prices
(t) Advance steel sector revival addressing Ajaokuta saga promoting local production despite decades of failure and massive resources invested without production

## 749. NIGERIAN INSTITUTE OF SOCIAL AND ECONOMIC RESEARCH (NISER), IBADAN

**MDA Code:** 238002001

Nigerian Institute of Social and Economic Research is premier federal think tank conducting economic and social policy research. Located in Ibadan, Oyo State, operating under Office of President. Established to conduct policy research, provide economic analysis and advise government on development issues. Conducts research on Nigerian economy and development. Researches poverty, inequality and social development. Studies economic policies and macroeconomic issues. Conducts labor market and employment research. Researches agriculture and rural development. Studies industrialization and manufacturing sector. Analyzes trade policies and competitiveness. Researches fiscal policy and public finance. Provides economic forecasting and projections. Advises government on economic policies. Publishes research papers and policy briefs. Collaborates with universities and international research institutes. Addresses challenges from limited funding, research-policy gap and implementation of recommendations. Think tank research often ignored by policymakers. Critical for evidence-based policy, economic analysis and providing research supporting Nigerian development despite research-policy implementation gaps.
